
=== DataProvider: Reportes.MenuReportesHC ===

=== WebPanel: Reportes.CarreteEnPallet ===

--- Start (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble_RangePicker.Attach(&DDO_PaletHoraFinEnsambleAuxDateText.InternalName)
	
	Grid.Rows = Page.Rows
	Grid_Empowerer.GridInternalName = Grid.InternalName
	DDO_GridColumnsSelector.GridInternalName = Grid.InternalName
	If &HTTPRequest.Method = HttpMethod.Get
		Do 'LoadSavedFilters'
	EndIf
	ddo_AGExport.TitleControlIdToReplace = BtnAGExport.InternalName
	&AGExportData = new()
	
	&AGExportDataItem = new()
	&AGExportDataItem.Title = ''
	&AGExportDataItem.Icon = ActionExport.Link()
	&AGExportDataItem.EventKey = !'Export'
	&AGExportDataItem.IsDivider = False
	&AGExportData.Add(&AGExportDataItem)
	
	&AGExportDataItem = new()
	&AGExportDataItem.Title = ''
	&AGExportDataItem.Icon = ActionExportReport.Link()
	&AGExportDataItem.EventKey = !'ExportReport'
	&AGExportDataItem.IsDivider = False
	&AGExportData.Add(&AGExportDataItem)
	
	&IsAuthorized_PaletNoSerie = SecGAMIsAuthByFunctionalityKey.Udp(!'paletview_Execute')
	
	&GAMSession = GAMSession.Get(&GAMErrors)
	DDO_Grid.GridInternalName = Grid.InternalName
	DDO_Grid.GAMOAuthToken = &GAMSession.Token
	Form.Caption = ' Palet Carrete'
	Do 'PrepareTransaction'
	Do 'LoadGridState'
	If &OrderedBy < 1
		&OrderedBy = 1
		Do 'SetDDOSortedStatus'
	EndIf
	&DDO_TitleSettingsIcons = GetWWPTitleSettingsIcons()
	DDO_GridColumnsSelector.TitleControlIdToReplace = BtnEditColumns.InternalName
	GridPaginationBar.RowsPerPageSelectedValue = Grid.Rows
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- Refresh (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	LoadWWPContext.Call(&WWPContext)
	Do Case
		Case &ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 1
			&ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 2
		Case &ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 2
			&ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 0
			Do 'LoadSavedFilters'
	EndCase
	Do 'SaveGridState'
	
	If &Session.Get(!'Reportes.CarreteEnPalletColumnsSelector') <> ''
		&ColumnsSelectorXML = &Session.Get(!'Reportes.CarreteEnPalletColumnsSelector')
		&ColumnsSelector.FromXml(&ColumnsSelectorXML)
	Else
		Do 'InitializeColumnsSelector'
	EndIf
	PaletHoraFinEnsamble.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(1).IsVisible
	PaletNoSerie.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(2).IsVisible
	PaletTipo.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(3).IsVisible
	PaletEstatus.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(4).IsVisible
	PaletProductoNombre.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(5).IsVisible
	CarreteNoSerie.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(6).IsVisible
	&GridCurrentPage = Grid.CurrentPage
	&GridPageCount = Grid.PageCount
	&GridAppliedFilters = WWP_GetAppliedFiltersDescription(&Pgmname)
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- GridPaginationBar.ChangePage (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Do Case
		Case GridPaginationBar.SelectedPage = !'Previous'
			Grid.PreviousPage()
		Case GridPaginationBar.SelectedPage = !'Next'
			Grid.NextPage()
		Otherwise
			&PageToGo.FromString(GridPaginationBar.SelectedPage)
			Grid.GotoPage(&PageToGo)
	EndCase
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- GridPaginationBar.ChangeRowsPerPage (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Grid.Rows = GridPaginationBar.RowsPerPageSelectedValue
	Grid.FirstPage()
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- DDO_Grid.OnOptionClicked (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Do Case
		Case DDO_Grid.ActiveEventKey = !'<#OrderASC#>' OR DDO_Grid.ActiveEventKey = !'<#OrderDSC#>'
			&OrderedBy.FromString(DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get)
			&OrderedDsc = iif(DDO_Grid.ActiveEventKey = !'<#OrderDSC#>', true, false)
			Do 'SetDDOSortedStatus'
			Grid.FirstPage()
		Case DDO_Grid.ActiveEventKey = !'<#Filter#>'
			Do Case
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'PaletHoraFinEnsamble'
					&TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get)
					&TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble_To.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredTextTo_get)
					If Not &TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble_To.IsEmpty()
						&TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble_To.Set(&TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble_To.Year(), &TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble_To.Month(), &TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble_To.Day(), 23, 59, 59)
					EndIf
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'PaletNoSerie'
					&TFPaletNoSerie = DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get
					&TFPaletNoSerie_Sel = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'PaletTipo'
					&TFPaletTipo_SelsJson = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
					&TFPaletTipo_Sels.FromJson(&TFPaletTipo_SelsJson)
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'PaletEstatus'
					&TFPaletEstatus_SelsJson = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
					&TFPaletEstatus_Sels.FromJson(&TFPaletEstatus_SelsJson)
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'PaletProductoNombre'
					&TFPaletProductoNombre = DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get
					&TFPaletProductoNombre_Sel = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'CarreteNoSerie'
					&TFCarreteNoSerie = DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get
					&TFCarreteNoSerie_Sel = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
			EndCase
			Grid.FirstPage()
	EndCase
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- Grid.Load (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	If (&IsAuthorized_PaletNoSerie)
		PaletNoSerie.Link = DB.PaletView.Link(PaletId, '')
	Endif
	Do Case
		Case PaletEstatus = EstatusPalet.Embarcado
			&GridBadge = "Embarcado"
			&GridBadge.TooltipText = "Embarcado"
			&GridBadge.Class = !' AttributeTagSuccess BootstrapTooltipRight'
		Case PaletEstatus = EstatusPalet.Terminado
			&GridBadge = "Terminado"
			&GridBadge.TooltipText = "Terminado"
			&GridBadge.Class = !' AttributeTagInfo BootstrapTooltipRight'
		Otherwise
			&GridBadge = ''
			&GridBadge.TooltipText = ''
			&GridBadge.Class = !''
	EndCase
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- DDO_GridColumnsSelector.OnColumnsChanged (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	&ColumnsSelectorXML = DDO_GridColumnsSelector.ColumnsSelectorValues
	&ColumnsSelector.FromJson(&ColumnsSelectorXML)
	SaveColumnsSelectorState(!'Reportes.CarreteEnPalletColumnsSelector', iif(&ColumnsSelectorXML.IsEmpty(), '', &ColumnsSelector.ToXml()))
	Refresh
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- ddo_ManageFilters.OnOptionClicked (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Do Case
		Case ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey = !'<#Clean#>'
			Do 'CleanFilters'
			Grid.FirstPage()
			Refresh
		Case ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey = !'<#Save#>'
			Do 'SaveGridState'
			SaveFilterAs.Popup(!'Reportes.CarreteEnPalletFilters', &PgmName + !"GridState")
			&ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 2
			Refresh
		Case ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey = !'<#Manage#>'
			ManageFilters.Popup(!'Reportes.CarreteEnPalletFilters')
			&ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 2
			Refresh
		Otherwise
			&ManageFiltersXml = GetFilterByName(!'Reportes.CarreteEnPalletFilters', ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey)
			If &ManageFiltersXml.IsEmpty()
				msg('WWP_FilterNotExist')
			Else
				Do 'CleanFilters'
				SaveGridState.Call(&PgmName + !"GridState",  &ManageFiltersXml)
				&GridState.FromXml(&ManageFiltersXml)
				&OrderedBy = &GridState.OrderedBy
				&OrderedDsc = &GridState.OrderedDsc
				Do 'SetDDOSortedStatus' 
				Do 'LoadRegFiltersState'
				Grid.FirstPage()
				Refresh
			EndIf
	EndCase
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- ddo_AGExport.OnOptionClicked (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Do Case
		Case ddo_AGExport.ActiveEventKey = !'Export'
			Do 'DoExport'
		Case ddo_AGExport.ActiveEventKey = !'ExportReport'
			Do 'DoExportReport'
	EndCase
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- Rules (Rules) ---

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */



	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */


=== WebPanel: Reportes.CausaInterrupcionWW ===

--- Start (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Grid.Rows = Page.Rows
	Grid_Empowerer.GridInternalName = Grid.InternalName
	DDO_GridColumnsSelector.GridInternalName = Grid.InternalName
	If &HTTPRequest.Method = HttpMethod.Get
		Do 'LoadSavedFilters'
	EndIf
	ddo_AGExport.TitleControlIdToReplace = BtnAGExport.InternalName
	&AGExportData = new()
	
	&AGExportDataItem = new()
	&AGExportDataItem.Title = ''
	&AGExportDataItem.Icon = ActionExport.Link()
	&AGExportDataItem.EventKey = !'Export'
	&AGExportDataItem.IsDivider = False
	&AGExportData.Add(&AGExportDataItem)
	
	&AGExportDataItem = new()
	&AGExportDataItem.Title = ''
	&AGExportDataItem.Icon = ActionExportReport.Link()
	&AGExportDataItem.EventKey = !'ExportReport'
	&AGExportDataItem.IsDivider = False
	&AGExportData.Add(&AGExportDataItem)
	
	DDC_Subscriptions.TitleControlIdToReplace = BtnSubscriptions.InternalName
	
	&IsAuthorized_CausaInterrupcionNombre = SecGAMIsAuthByFunctionalityKey.Udp(!'causainterrupcionview_Execute')
	
	&GAMSession = GAMSession.Get(&GAMErrors)
	DDO_Grid.GridInternalName = Grid.InternalName
	DDO_Grid.GAMOAuthToken = &GAMSession.Token
	Form.Caption = ' Causa Interrupcion'
	Do 'PrepareTransaction'
	Do 'LoadGridState'
	If &OrderedBy < 1
		&OrderedBy = 1
		Do 'SetDDOSortedStatus'
	EndIf
	&DDO_TitleSettingsIcons = GetWWPTitleSettingsIcons()
	DDO_GridColumnsSelector.TitleControlIdToReplace = BtnEditColumns.InternalName
	GridPaginationBar.RowsPerPageSelectedValue = Grid.Rows
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- Refresh (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	LoadWWPContext.Call(&WWPContext)
	Do 'CheckSecurityForActions'
	Do Case
		Case &ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 1
			&ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 2
		Case &ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 2
			&ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 0
			Do 'LoadSavedFilters'
	EndCase
	Do 'SaveGridState'
	
	If &Session.Get(!'Reportes.CausaInterrupcionWWColumnsSelector') <> ''
		&ColumnsSelectorXML = &Session.Get(!'Reportes.CausaInterrupcionWWColumnsSelector')
		&ColumnsSelector.FromXml(&ColumnsSelectorXML)
	Else
		Do 'InitializeColumnsSelector'
	EndIf
	CausaInterrupcionNombre.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(1).IsVisible
	CausaInterrupcionPrensa.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(2).IsVisible
	CausaInterrupcionExtrusora.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(3).IsVisible
	&GridCurrentPage = Grid.CurrentPage
	&GridPageCount = Grid.PageCount
	&GridAppliedFilters = WWP_GetAppliedFiltersDescription(&Pgmname)
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- GridPaginationBar.ChangePage (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Do Case
		Case GridPaginationBar.SelectedPage = !'Previous'
			Grid.PreviousPage()
		Case GridPaginationBar.SelectedPage = !'Next'
			Grid.NextPage()
		Otherwise
			&PageToGo.FromString(GridPaginationBar.SelectedPage)
			Grid.GotoPage(&PageToGo)
	EndCase
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- GridPaginationBar.ChangeRowsPerPage (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Grid.Rows = GridPaginationBar.RowsPerPageSelectedValue
	Grid.FirstPage()
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- DDO_Grid.OnOptionClicked (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Do Case
		Case DDO_Grid.ActiveEventKey = !'<#OrderASC#>' OR DDO_Grid.ActiveEventKey = !'<#OrderDSC#>'
			&OrderedBy.FromString(DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get)
			&OrderedDsc = iif(DDO_Grid.ActiveEventKey = !'<#OrderDSC#>', true, false)
			Do 'SetDDOSortedStatus'
			Grid.FirstPage()
		Case DDO_Grid.ActiveEventKey = !'<#Filter#>'
			Do Case
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'CausaInterrupcionNombre'
					&TFCausaInterrupcionNombre = DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get
					&TFCausaInterrupcionNombre_Sel = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'CausaInterrupcionPrensa'
					&TFCausaInterrupcionPrensa_Sel.FromString(DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get)
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'CausaInterrupcionExtrusora'
					&TFCausaInterrupcionExtrusora_Sel.FromString(DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get)
			EndCase
			Grid.FirstPage()
	EndCase
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- Grid.Load (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	&Update = !'<i class="fa fa-pen"></i>'
	If (&IsAuthorized_Update)
		&Update.Link = Reportes.CausaInterrupcion.Link(TrnMode.Update, CausaInterrupcionId)
	Endif
	&Delete = !'<i class="fa fa-times"></i>'
	If (&IsAuthorized_Delete)
		&Delete.Link = Reportes.CausaInterrupcion.Link(TrnMode.Delete, CausaInterrupcionId)
	Endif
	If (&IsAuthorized_CausaInterrupcionNombre)
		CausaInterrupcionNombre.Link = Reportes.CausaInterrupcionView.Link(CausaInterrupcionId, '')
	Endif
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- DDO_GridColumnsSelector.OnColumnsChanged (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	&ColumnsSelectorXML = DDO_GridColumnsSelector.ColumnsSelectorValues
	&ColumnsSelector.FromJson(&ColumnsSelectorXML)
	SaveColumnsSelectorState(!'Reportes.CausaInterrupcionWWColumnsSelector', iif(&ColumnsSelectorXML.IsEmpty(), '', &ColumnsSelector.ToXml()))
	Refresh
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- ddo_ManageFilters.OnOptionClicked (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Do Case
		Case ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey = !'<#Clean#>'
			Do 'CleanFilters'
			Grid.FirstPage()
			Refresh
		Case ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey = !'<#Save#>'
			Do 'SaveGridState'
			SaveFilterAs.Popup(!'Reportes.CausaInterrupcionWWFilters', &PgmName + !"GridState")
			&ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 2
			Refresh
		Case ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey = !'<#Manage#>'
			ManageFilters.Popup(!'Reportes.CausaInterrupcionWWFilters')
			&ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 2
			Refresh
		Otherwise
			&ManageFiltersXml = GetFilterByName(!'Reportes.CausaInterrupcionWWFilters', ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey)
			If &ManageFiltersXml.IsEmpty()
				msg('WWP_FilterNotExist')
			Else
				Do 'CleanFilters'
				SaveGridState.Call(&PgmName + !"GridState",  &ManageFiltersXml)
				&GridState.FromXml(&ManageFiltersXml)
				&OrderedBy = &GridState.OrderedBy
				&OrderedDsc = &GridState.OrderedDsc
				Do 'SetDDOSortedStatus' 
				Do 'LoadRegFiltersState'
				Grid.FirstPage()
				Refresh
			EndIf
	EndCase
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- 'DoInsert' (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	If (&IsAuthorized_Insert)
		Reportes.CausaInterrupcion.Call(TrnMode.Insert, nullvalue(CausaInterrupcionId))
	Else
		msg("WWP_ActionNoLongerAvailable")
		Refresh
	Endif
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- ddo_AGExport.OnOptionClicked (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Do Case
		Case ddo_AGExport.ActiveEventKey = !'Export'
			Do 'DoExport'
		Case ddo_AGExport.ActiveEventKey = !'ExportReport'
			Do 'DoExportReport'
	EndCase
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- DDC_Subscriptions.OnLoadComponent (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	WWPAux_WC.Object = WWP_SubscriptionsPanel.Create(!'CausaInterrupcion', WWPBaseObjects.Notifications.WWP_NotificationAppliesTo.AnyRecord, '', '')
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- Rules (Rules) ---

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */



	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */


=== WebPanel: Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacionWW ===

--- Start (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	TFExtrusoraObservacionFecha_RangePicker.Attach(&DDO_ExtrusoraObservacionFechaAuxDateText.InternalName)
	
	Grid.Rows = Page.Rows
	Grid_Empowerer.GridInternalName = Grid.InternalName
	DDO_GridColumnsSelector.GridInternalName = Grid.InternalName
	If &HTTPRequest.Method = HttpMethod.Get
		Do 'LoadSavedFilters'
	EndIf

	&AGExportData = new()
	
	&AGExportDataItem = new()

	&AGExportDataItem.Icon = ActionExport.Link()
	&AGExportDataItem.EventKey = !'Export'
	&AGExportDataItem.IsDivider = False
	&AGExportData.Add(&AGExportDataItem)
	
	&AGExportDataItem = new()
	&AGExportDataItem.Title = ''
	&AGExportDataItem.Icon = ActionExportReport.Link()
	&AGExportDataItem.EventKey = !'ExportReport'
	&AGExportDataItem.IsDivider = False
	&AGExportData.Add(&AGExportDataItem)
	
	DDC_Subscriptions.TitleControlIdToReplace = BtnSubscriptions.InternalName

	DDO_GridColumnsSelector.TitleControlIdToReplace = BtnEditColumns.InternalName
	GridPaginationBar.RowsPerPageSelectedValue = Grid.Rows
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- Refresh (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	LoadWWPContext.Call(&WWPContext)
	Do 'CheckSecurityForActions'
	Do Case
		Case &ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 1
			&ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 2
		Case &ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 2
			&ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 0
			Do 'LoadSavedFilters'
	EndCase
	Do 'SaveGridState'
	
	If &Session.Get(!'Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacionWWColumnsSelector') <> ''
		&ColumnsSelectorXML = &Session.Get(!'Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacionWWColumnsSelector')
		&ColumnsSelector.FromXml(&ColumnsSelectorXML)
	Else
		Do 'InitializeColumnsSelector'
	EndIf
	ExtrusoraObservacionFecha.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(1).IsVisible
	ExtrusoraNombre.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(2).IsVisible
	TurnoNombre.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(3).IsVisible
	ExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(4).IsVisible
	ExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(5).IsVisible
	ExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(6).IsVisible
	&GridCurrentPage = Grid.CurrentPage
	&GridPageCount = Grid.PageCount
	&GridAppliedFilters = WWP_GetAppliedFiltersDescription(&Pgmname)
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- GridPaginationBar.ChangePage (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Do Case
		Case GridPaginationBar.SelectedPage = !'Previous'
			Grid.PreviousPage()
		Case GridPaginationBar.SelectedPage = !'Next'
			Grid.NextPage()
		Otherwise
			&PageToGo.FromString(GridPaginationBar.SelectedPage)
			Grid.GotoPage(&PageToGo)
	EndCase
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- GridPaginationBar.ChangeRowsPerPage (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Grid.Rows = GridPaginationBar.RowsPerPageSelectedValue
	Grid.FirstPage()
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- DDO_Grid.OnOptionClicked (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Do Case
		Case DDO_Grid.ActiveEventKey = !'<#OrderASC#>' OR DDO_Grid.ActiveEventKey = !'<#OrderDSC#>'
			&OrderedBy.FromString(DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get)
			&OrderedDsc = iif(DDO_Grid.ActiveEventKey = !'<#OrderDSC#>', true, false)
			Do 'SetDDOSortedStatus'
			Grid.FirstPage()
		Case DDO_Grid.ActiveEventKey = !'<#Filter#>'
			Do Case
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusoraObservacionFecha'
					&TFExtrusoraObservacionFecha.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get)
					&TFExtrusoraObservacionFecha_To.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredTextTo_get)
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusoraNombre'
					&TFExtrusoraNombre = DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get
					&TFExtrusoraNombre_Sel = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'TurnoNombre'
					&TFTurnoNombre = DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get
					&TFTurnoNombre_Sel = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion'
					&TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get)
					&TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredTextTo_get)
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor'
					&TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get)
					&TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredTextTo_get)
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion'
					&TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion = DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get
					&TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion_Sel = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
			EndCase
			Grid.FirstPage()
	EndCase
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- Grid.Load (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	&Update = !'<i class="fa fa-pen"></i>'
	If (&IsAuthorized_Update)
		&Update.Link = Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacion.Link(TrnMode.Update, ExtrusoraObservacionId)
	Endif
	&Delete = !'<i class="fa fa-times"></i>'
	If (&IsAuthorized_Delete)
		&Delete.Link = Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacion.Link(TrnMode.Delete, ExtrusoraObservacionId)
	Endif
	&GridActions.Clear()
	&GridActions.AddItem(0, !";fa fa-bars")
	If (&IsAuthorized_Display)
		&GridActions.AddItem(1, format(!'%1;%2', "GXM_display", !"fa fa-search"))
	Endif
	If (&IsAuthorized_Update)
		&GridActions.AddItem(2, format(!'%1;%2', "GXM_update", !"fa fa-pen"))
	Endif
	If (&IsAuthorized_Delete)
		&GridActions.AddItem(3, format(!'%1;%2', "GX_BtnDelete", !"fa fa-times"))
	Endif
	If &GridActions.Count = 1
		&GridActions.Class = !'Invisible'
	Else
		&GridActions.Class = !'ConvertToDDO'
	EndIf
	
	If (&IsAuthorized_ExtrusoraObservacionFecha)
		ExtrusoraObservacionFecha.Link = ExtrusoraObservacionView.Link(ExtrusoraObservacionId, '')
	Endif
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- DDO_GridColumnsSelector.OnColumnsChanged (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	&ColumnsSelectorXML = DDO_GridColumnsSelector.ColumnsSelectorValues
	&ColumnsSelector.FromJson(&ColumnsSelectorXML)
	SaveColumnsSelectorState(!'Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacionWWColumnsSelector', iif(&ColumnsSelectorXML.IsEmpty(), '', &ColumnsSelector.ToXml()))
	Refresh
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- ddo_ManageFilters.OnOptionClicked (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Do Case
		Case ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey = !'<#Clean#>'
			Do 'CleanFilters'
			Grid.FirstPage()
			Refresh
		Case ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey = !'<#Save#>'
			Do 'SaveGridState'
			SaveFilterAs.Popup(!'Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacionWWFilters', &PgmName + !"GridState")
			&ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 2
			Refresh
		Case ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey = !'<#Manage#>'
			ManageFilters.Popup(!'Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacionWWFilters')
			&ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 2
			Refresh
		Otherwise
			&ManageFiltersXml = GetFilterByName(!'Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacionWWFilters', ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey)
			If &ManageFiltersXml.IsEmpty()
				msg('WWP_FilterNotExist')
			Else
				Do 'CleanFilters'
				SaveGridState.Call(&PgmName + !"GridState",  &ManageFiltersXml)
				&GridState.FromXml(&ManageFiltersXml)
				&OrderedBy = &GridState.OrderedBy
				&OrderedDsc = &GridState.OrderedDsc
				Do 'SetDDOSortedStatus' 
				Do 'LoadRegFiltersState'
				Grid.FirstPage()
				Refresh
			EndIf
	EndCase
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- &GridActions.Click (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Do Case
		Case &GridActions = 1
			Do 'Do Display'
		Case &GridActions = 2
			Do 'Do Update'
		Case &GridActions = 3
			Do 'Do Delete'
	EndCase
	&GridActions = 0
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- 'DoInsert' (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	If (&IsAuthorized_Insert)
		Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacion.Call(TrnMode.Insert, nullvalue(ExtrusoraObservacionId))
	Else
		msg("WWP_ActionNoLongerAvailable")
		Refresh
	Endif
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- ddo_AGExport.OnOptionClicked (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Do Case
		Case ddo_AGExport.ActiveEventKey = !'Export'
			Do 'DoExport'
		Case ddo_AGExport.ActiveEventKey = !'ExportReport'
			Do 'DoExportReport'
	EndCase
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- DDC_Subscriptions.OnLoadComponent (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	WWPAux_WC.Object = WWP_SubscriptionsPanel.Create(!'ExtrusoraObservacion', WWPBaseObjects.Notifications.WWP_NotificationAppliesTo.AnyRecord, '', '')
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- Rules (Rules) ---

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */



	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

	Endif
	&Delete = !'<i class="fa fa-times"></i>'
	If (&IsAuthorized_Delete)
		&Delete.Link = Reportes.PrensaObservacion.Link(TrnMode.Delete, PrensaObservacionId)
	Endif
	If (&IsAuthorized_PrensaObservacionFecha)
		PrensaObservacionFecha.Link = Reportes.PrensaObservacionView.Link(PrensaObservacionId, '')
	Endif
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- DDO_GridColumnsSelector.OnColumnsChanged (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	&ColumnsSelectorXML = DDO_GridColumnsSelector.ColumnsSelectorValues
	&ColumnsSelector.FromJson(&ColumnsSelectorXML)
	SaveColumnsSelectorState(!'Reportes.PrensaObservacionWWColumnsSelector', iif(&ColumnsSelectorXML.IsEmpty(), '', &ColumnsSelector.ToXml()))
	Refresh
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- ddo_ManageFilters.OnOptionClicked (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Do Case
		Case ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey = !'<#Clean#>'
			Do 'CleanFilters'
			Grid.FirstPage()
			Refresh
		Case ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey = !'<#Save#>'
			Do 'SaveGridState'
			SaveFilterAs.Popup(!'Reportes.PrensaObservacionWWFilters', &PgmName + !"GridState")
			&ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 2
			Refresh
		Case ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey = !'<#Manage#>'
			ManageFilters.Popup(!'Reportes.PrensaObservacionWWFilters')
			&ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 2
			Refresh
		Otherwise
			&ManageFiltersXml = GetFilterByName(!'Reportes.PrensaObservacionWWFilters', ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey)
			If &ManageFiltersXml.IsEmpty()
				msg('WWP_FilterNotExist')
			Else
				Do 'CleanFilters'
				SaveGridState.Call(&PgmName + !"GridState",  &ManageFiltersXml)
				&GridState.FromXml(&ManageFiltersXml)
				&OrderedBy = &GridState.OrderedBy
				&OrderedDsc = &GridState.OrderedDsc
				Do 'SetDDOSortedStatus' 
				Do 'LoadRegFiltersState'
				Grid.FirstPage()
				Refresh
			EndIf
	EndCase
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- 'DoInsert' (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	If (&IsAuthorized_Insert)
		Reportes.PrensaObservacion.Call(TrnMode.Insert, nullvalue(PrensaObservacionId))
	Else
		msg("WWP_ActionNoLongerAvailable")
		Refresh
	Endif
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- ddo_AGExport.OnOptionClicked (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Do Case
		Case ddo_AGExport.ActiveEventKey = !'Export'
			Do 'DoExport'
		Case ddo_AGExport.ActiveEventKey = !'ExportReport'
			Do 'DoExportReport'
	EndCase
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- DDC_Subscriptions.OnLoadComponent (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	WWPAux_WC.Object = WWP_SubscriptionsPanel.Create(!'PrensaObservacion', WWPBaseObjects.Notifications.WWP_NotificationAppliesTo.AnyRecord, '', '')
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- Rules (Rules) ---

/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	TFExtrusionFecha_RangePicker.Attach(&DDO_ExtrusionFechaAuxDateText.InternalName)

	&AGExportData.Add(&AGExportDataItem)

	LoadWWPContext.Call(&WWPContext)
	Do 'CheckSecurityForActions'
	Do Case
		Case &ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 1
			&ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 2
		Case &ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 2
			&ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 0
			Do 'LoadSavedFilters'
	EndCase
	Do 'SaveGridState'
	
	If &Session.Get(!'Reportes.vwExtrusionResultadoColumnsSelector') <> ''
		&ColumnsSelectorXML = &Session.Get(!'Reportes.vwExtrusionResultadoColumnsSelector')

	Else

	EndIf
	ExtrusionFecha.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(1).IsVisible
	ExtrusionExtrusoraNombre.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(2).IsVisible
	ExtrusionProductoNombre.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(3).IsVisible
	ExtrusionTurnoNombre.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(4).IsVisible

	ExtrusionResultadoVelLaminadora.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(8).IsVisible

	ExtrusionResultadoTotalKg.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(10).IsVisible
	ExtrusionResultadoTotalMermaKg.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(11).IsVisible
	ExtrusionResultadoCOMBA.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(12).IsVisible
	ExtrusionResultadoObservaciones.Visible = &ColumnsSelector.Columns.Item(13).IsVisible

--- GridPaginationBar.ChangePage (Event) ---

		Case GridPaginationBar.SelectedPage = !'Previous'
			Grid.PreviousPage()
		Case GridPaginationBar.SelectedPage = !'Next'
			Grid.NextPage()
		Otherwise

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- DDO_Grid.OnOptionClicked (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Do Case
		Case DDO_Grid.ActiveEventKey = !'<#OrderASC#>' OR DDO_Grid.ActiveEventKey = !'<#OrderDSC#>'
			&OrderedBy.FromString(DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get)
			&OrderedDsc = iif(DDO_Grid.ActiveEventKey = !'<#OrderDSC#>', true, false)
			Do 'SetDDOSortedStatus'
			Grid.FirstPage()
		Case DDO_Grid.ActiveEventKey = !'<#Filter#>'
			Do Case
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusionFecha'
					&TFExtrusionFecha.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get)
					&TFExtrusionFecha_To.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredTextTo_get)
					If Not &TFExtrusionFecha_To.IsEmpty()
						&TFExtrusionFecha_To.Set(&TFExtrusionFecha_To.Year(), &TFExtrusionFecha_To.Month(), &TFExtrusionFecha_To.Day(), 23, 59, 59)
					EndIf
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusionExtrusoraNombre'
					&TFExtrusionExtrusoraNombre = DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get
					&TFExtrusionExtrusoraNombre_Sel = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusionProductoNombre'
					&TFExtrusionProductoNombre = DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get
					&TFExtrusionProductoNombre_Sel = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusionTurnoNombre'
					&TFExtrusionTurnoNombre = DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get
					&TFExtrusionTurnoNombre_Sel = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusionOperadorNombre'
					&TFExtrusionOperadorNombre = DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get
					&TFExtrusionOperadorNombre_Sel = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusionResultadoBobinasMolino'
					&TFExtrusionResultadoBobinasMolino.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get)
					&TFExtrusionResultadoBobinasMolino_To.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredTextTo_get)
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusionResultadoBobinasReposo'
					&TFExtrusionResultadoBobinasReposo.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get)
					&TFExtrusionResultadoBobinasReposo_To.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredTextTo_get)
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusionResultadoVelLaminadora'
					&TFExtrusionResultadoVelLaminadora.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get)
					&TFExtrusionResultadoVelLaminadora_To.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredTextTo_get)
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusionResultadoVelHusillo'
					&TFExtrusionResultadoVelHusillo.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get)
					&TFExtrusionResultadoVelHusillo_To.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredTextTo_get)
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusionResultadoTotalKg'
					&TFExtrusionResultadoTotalKg.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get)
					&TFExtrusionResultadoTotalKg_To.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredTextTo_get)
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusionResultadoTotalMermaKg'
					&TFExtrusionResultadoTotalMermaKg.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get)
					&TFExtrusionResultadoTotalMermaKg_To.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredTextTo_get)
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusionResultadoCOMBA'
					&TFExtrusionResultadoCOMBA_Sel.FromString(DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get)
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusionResultadoObservaciones'
					&TFExtrusionResultadoObservaciones = DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get
					&TFExtrusionResultadoObservaciones_Sel = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusionSiloNombre'
					&TFExtrusionSiloNombre = DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get
					&TFExtrusionSiloNombre_Sel = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusionLoteSilo'
					&TFExtrusionLoteSilo = DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get
					&TFExtrusionLoteSilo_Sel = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusionRevHusilloMolido'
					&TFExtrusionRevHusilloMolido.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get)
					&TFExtrusionRevHusilloMolido_To.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredTextTo_get)
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusionRevHusilloVirgen'
					&TFExtrusionRevHusilloVirgen.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get)
					&TFExtrusionRevHusilloVirgen_To.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredTextTo_get)
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusionVirgenKg'
					&TFExtrusionVirgenKg.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get)
					&TFExtrusionVirgenKg_To.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredTextTo_get)
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'ExtrusionMolidoKg'
					&TFExtrusionMolidoKg.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get)
					&TFExtrusionMolidoKg_To.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredTextTo_get)
			EndCase
			Grid.FirstPage()
	EndCase
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- Grid.Load (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	&ReporteExtrusion = !'<i class="fas fa-print"></i>'
	
	&DetailWebComponent = !'<i class="fas fa-info-circle"></i>'
	If (ExtrusionResultadoBobinasMolino > 0)

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- ddo_ManageFilters.OnOptionClicked (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Do Case
		Case ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey = !'<#Clean#>'
			Do 'CleanFilters'
			Grid.FirstPage()
			Refresh
		Case ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey = !'<#Save#>'
			Do 'SaveGridState'
			SaveFilterAs.Popup(!'Reportes.vwExtrusionResultadoFilters', &PgmName + !"GridState")
			&ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 2
			Refresh
		Case ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey = !'<#Manage#>'
			ManageFilters.Popup(!'Reportes.vwExtrusionResultadoFilters')
			&ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 2
			Refresh
		Otherwise
			&ManageFiltersXml = GetFilterByName(!'Reportes.vwExtrusionResultadoFilters', ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey)
			If &ManageFiltersXml.IsEmpty()
				msg('WWP_FilterNotExist')
			Else
				Do 'CleanFilters'
				SaveGridState.Call(&PgmName + !"GridState",  &ManageFiltersXml)
				&GridState.FromXml(&ManageFiltersXml)
				&OrderedBy = &GridState.OrderedBy
				&OrderedDsc = &GridState.OrderedDsc
				Do 'SetDDOSortedStatus' 
				Do 'LoadRegFiltersState'
				Grid.FirstPage()
				Refresh
			EndIf
	EndCase
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

		Refresh
	Else
		msg("WWP_ActionNoLongerAvailable")
		Refresh
	Endif

	Do Case
		Case DDO_Grid.ActiveEventKey = !'<#OrderASC#>' OR DDO_Grid.ActiveEventKey = !'<#OrderDSC#>'
			&OrderedBy.FromString(DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get)
			&OrderedDsc = iif(DDO_Grid.ActiveEventKey = !'<#OrderDSC#>', true, false)

				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'OrdenEtiquetadoFechaInicio'
					&TFOrdenEtiquetadoFechaInicio.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get)
					&TFOrdenEtiquetadoFechaInicio_To.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredTextTo_get)
					If Not &TFOrdenEtiquetadoFechaInicio_To.IsEmpty()
						&TFOrdenEtiquetadoFechaInicio_To.Set(&TFOrdenEtiquetadoFechaInicio_To.Year(), &TFOrdenEtiquetadoFechaInicio_To.Month(), &TFOrdenEtiquetadoFechaInicio_To.Day(), 23, 59, 59)
					EndIf
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'OrdenEtiquetadoFechaTermina'
					&TFOrdenEtiquetadoFechaTermina.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get)
					&TFOrdenEtiquetadoFechaTermina_To.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredTextTo_get)
					If Not &TFOrdenEtiquetadoFechaTermina_To.IsEmpty()
						&TFOrdenEtiquetadoFechaTermina_To.Set(&TFOrdenEtiquetadoFechaTermina_To.Year(), &TFOrdenEtiquetadoFechaTermina_To.Month(), &TFOrdenEtiquetadoFechaTermina_To.Day(), 23, 59, 59)
					EndIf
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'OperadorEtiquetadoNombre'
					&TFOperadorEtiquetadoNombre = DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get
					&TFOperadorEtiquetadoNombre_Sel = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'TurnoEtiquetadoNombre'
					&TFTurnoEtiquetadoNombre = DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get
					&TFTurnoEtiquetadoNombre_Sel = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'OrdenEtiquetadoPiezasBuenas'
					&TFOrdenEtiquetadoPiezasBuenas.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get)
					&TFOrdenEtiquetadoPiezasBuenas_To.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredTextTo_get)
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'OrdenEtiquetadoPiezasMolino'
					&TFOrdenEtiquetadoPiezasMolino.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get)
					&TFOrdenEtiquetadoPiezasMolino_To.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredTextTo_get)
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'OrdenEtiquetadoEtiquetadoraActiva'
					&TFOrdenEtiquetadoEtiquetadoraActiva = DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get
					&TFOrdenEtiquetadoEtiquetadoraActiva_Sel = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'OrdenEtiquetadoVelLineaUno'
					&TFOrdenEtiquetadoVelLineaUno = DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get
					&TFOrdenEtiquetadoVelLineaUno_Sel = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'OrdenEtiquetadoVelLineaDos'
					&TFOrdenEtiquetadoVelLineaDos = DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get
					&TFOrdenEtiquetadoVelLineaDos_Sel = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'OrdenEtiquetadoHorasUtiles'
					&TFOrdenEtiquetadoHorasUtiles.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get)
					&TFOrdenEtiquetadoHorasUtiles_To.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredTextTo_get)
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'OrdenEtiquetadoEficiencia'
					&TFOrdenEtiquetadoEficiencia.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get)
					&TFOrdenEtiquetadoEficiencia_To.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredTextTo_get)
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'OrdenEtiquetadoObservaciones'
					&TFOrdenEtiquetadoObservaciones = DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get
					&TFOrdenEtiquetadoObservaciones_Sel = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
			EndCase
			Grid.FirstPage()
	EndCase
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- Grid.Load (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	&ReporteEtiquetado = !'<i class="fas fa-print"></i>'
	If &IsAuthorized_ReporteEtiquetado
		If (OrdenEtiquetadoEstado = EstadoOrdenEtiquetado.Cerrada)
			&ReporteEtiquetado.Class = !'Attribute'
		Else
			&ReporteEtiquetado.Class = !'Invisible'
		Endif
	EndIf
	&Update = !'<i class="fa fa-pen"></i>'
	If (&IsAuthorized_Update)
		&Update.Link = DB.OrdenEtiquetado.Link(TrnMode.Update, OrdenEtiquetadoId)
	Endif
	&Delete = !'<i class="fa fa-times"></i>'
	If (&IsAuthorized_Delete)
		&Delete.Link = DB.OrdenEtiquetado.Link(TrnMode.Delete, OrdenEtiquetadoId)
	Endif
	Do Case
		Case OrdenEtiquetadoEstado = EstadoOrdenEtiquetado.Abierta
			&GridBadge = "Abierta"
			&GridBadge.TooltipText = "Abierta"
			&GridBadge.Class = !' AttributeTagWarning BootstrapTooltipRight'
		Case OrdenEtiquetadoEstado = EstadoOrdenEtiquetado.Cerrada
			&GridBadge = "Cerrada"
			&GridBadge.TooltipText = "Cerrada"
			&GridBadge.Class = !' AttributeTagSuccess BootstrapTooltipRight'
		Otherwise
			&GridBadge = ''
			&GridBadge.TooltipText = ''
			&GridBadge.Class = !''
	EndCase
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- DDO_GridColumnsSelector.OnColumnsChanged (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	&ColumnsSelectorXML = DDO_GridColumnsSelector.ColumnsSelectorValues
	&ColumnsSelector.FromJson(&ColumnsSelectorXML)
	WWPBaseObjects.SaveColumnsSelectorState(!'Reportes.vwOrdenEtiquetadoColumnsSelector', iif(&ColumnsSelectorXML.IsEmpty(), '', &ColumnsSelector.ToXml()))
	Refresh
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- ddo_ManageFilters.OnOptionClicked (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Do Case
		Case ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey = !'<#Clean#>'
			Do 'CleanFilters'
			Grid.FirstPage()
			Refresh
		Case ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey = !'<#Save#>'
			Do 'SaveGridState'
			SaveFilterAs.Popup(!'Reportes.vwOrdenEtiquetadoFilters', &PgmName + !"GridState")
			&ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 2
			Refresh
		Case ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey = !'<#Manage#>'
			ManageFilters.Popup(!'Reportes.vwOrdenEtiquetadoFilters')
			&ManageFiltersExecutionStep = 2
			Refresh
		Otherwise
			&ManageFiltersXml = GetFilterByName(!'Reportes.vwOrdenEtiquetadoFilters', ddo_ManageFilters.ActiveEventKey)
			If &ManageFiltersXml.IsEmpty()
				msg('WWP_FilterNotExist')
			Else
				Do 'CleanFilters'
				SaveGridState.Call(&PgmName + !"GridState",  &ManageFiltersXml)
				&GridState.FromXml(&ManageFiltersXml)
				&OrderedBy = &GridState.OrderedBy
				&OrderedDsc = &GridState.OrderedDsc
				Do 'SetDDOSortedStatus' 
				Do 'LoadRegFiltersState'
				Grid.FirstPage()
				Refresh
			EndIf
	EndCase
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- &ReporteEtiquetado.Click (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	If (&IsAuthorized_ReporteEtiquetado)
		EtiquetadoFormato.PopUp(OrdenEtiquetadoId)
		Refresh
	Else
		msg("WWP_ActionNoLongerAvailable")
		Refresh
	Endif
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- ddo_AGExport.OnOptionClicked (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	Do Case
		Case ddo_AGExport.ActiveEventKey = !'Export'
			Do 'DoExport'
		Case ddo_AGExport.ActiveEventKey = !'ExportReport'
			Do 'DoExportReport'
	EndCase
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

	WWPAux_WC.Object = WWP_SubscriptionsPanel.Create(!'OrdenEtiquetado', WWPBaseObjects.Notifications.WWP_NotificationAppliesTo.AnyRecord, '', '')
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

					&TFPrensadoResultadoCarretesSobrantes_To.FromString(DDO_Grid.FilteredTextTo_get)
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'PrensadoResultadoObservaciones'

					&TFPrensadoResultadoObservaciones_Sel = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'PrensadoResultadoRPMLinea'
					&TFPrensadoResultadoRPMLinea = DDO_Grid.FilteredText_get
					&TFPrensadoResultadoRPMLinea_Sel = DDO_Grid.SelectedValue_get
				Case DDO_Grid.SelectedColumn = !'PrensadoResultadoGPMPrensa'

					&TFPrensadoRodillosUnidadMedida_Sels.FromJson(&TFPrensadoRodillosUnidadMedida_SelsJson)

{
parm(in:&Mode, in:&EmbarqueDetalleId, in:&EmbarquePalletNoPallet);

EmbarqueDetalleId = &EmbarqueDetalleId if not &EmbarqueDetalleId.IsEmpty();
noaccept(EmbarqueDetalleId) if not &EmbarqueDetalleId.IsEmpty();
EmbarquePalletNoPallet = &EmbarquePalletNoPallet if not &EmbarquePalletNoPallet.IsEmpty();
noaccept(EmbarquePalletNoPallet) if not &EmbarquePalletNoPallet.IsEmpty();

}

=== Procedure: Reportes.CarreteEnPalletExport ===

--- Source (Source) ---

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

// Exports the contents of a grid (using the selected order and filters) to an Excel file.
WWPBaseObjects.LoadWWPContext.Call(&WWPContext)

Do 'OpenDocument'

&CellRow = 1
&FirstColumn = 1

Do 'LoadGridState'

Do 'WriteFilters'

Do 'WriteColumnTitles'

Do 'WriteData'

Do 'CloseDocument'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */


Sub 'OpenDocument'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&Random = Random() * 10000
	&Filename = !"CarreteEnPalletExport-" + &Random.ToString().Trim() + !".xlsx"

	&ExcelDocument.Open(&Filename)
	Do 'CheckStatus'
	&ExcelDocument.Clear()

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'WriteFilters'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	If not (&FilterFullText.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "WWP_FullTextFilterDescription")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&FilterFullText)
	EndIf
	If not (&TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble.IsEmpty() AND &TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble_To.IsEmpty())
		WWPBaseObjects.WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Fecha Terminado (Pallet)")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Date = &TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, false, &CellRow, &FirstColumn + 2, "WWP_TSTo")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 3).Date = &TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble_To
	EndIf
	If not (&TFPaletNoSerie_Sel.IsEmpty())
		WWPBaseObjects.WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Pallet")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFPaletNoSerie_Sel)
	Else
		If not (&TFPaletNoSerie.IsEmpty())
			WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Pallet")
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFPaletNoSerie)
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If not (&TFPaletTipo_Sels.Count = 0)
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Tipo")
		&i = 1
		For &TFPaletTipo_Sel in &TFPaletTipo_Sels
			If &i = 1
				&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = ''
			Else
				&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text += ', '
			EndIf
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text += &TFPaletTipo_Sel.EnumerationDescription()
			&i += 1
		EndFor
	EndIf
	If not (&TFPaletEstatus_Sels.Count = 0)
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Estatus")
		&i = 1
		For &TFPaletEstatus_Sel in &TFPaletEstatus_Sels
			If &i = 1
				&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = ''
			Else
				&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text += ', '
			EndIf
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text += &TFPaletEstatus_Sel.EnumerationDescription()
			&i += 1
		EndFor
	EndIf
	If not (&TFPaletProductoNombre_Sel.IsEmpty())
		WWPBaseObjects.WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Producto")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFPaletProductoNombre_Sel)
	Else
		If not (&TFPaletProductoNombre.IsEmpty())
			WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Producto")
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFPaletProductoNombre)
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If not (&TFCarreteNoSerie_Sel.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Carrete")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFCarreteNoSerie_Sel)
	Else
		If not (&TFCarreteNoSerie.IsEmpty())
			WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Carrete")
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFCarreteNoSerie)
		EndIf
	EndIf
	&CellRow += 2

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'WriteColumnTitles'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&VisibleColumnCount = 0
	If &Session.Get(!'Reportes.CarreteEnPalletColumnsSelector') <> ''
		&ColumnsSelectorXML = &Session.Get(!'Reportes.CarreteEnPalletColumnsSelector')
		&ColumnsSelector.FromXml(&ColumnsSelectorXML)
	Else
		Do 'InitializeColumnsSelector'
	EndIf

	&ColumnsSelector.Columns.Sort(!'Order')
	For &ColumnsSelector_Column in &ColumnsSelector.Columns
		If &ColumnsSelector_Column.IsVisible = True
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = GetMessageText(iif(&ColumnsSelector_Column.DisplayName.IsEmpty(), &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName, &ColumnsSelector_Column.DisplayName))
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Bold = True
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Color = 11
			&VisibleColumnCount += 1
		EndIf
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'WriteData'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	For each DB.PaletCarrete
		order DB.PaletCarreteId  when &OrderedBy = 1
		order PaletHoraFinEnsamble  when &OrderedBy = 2 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (WWPBaseObjects.PaletHoraFinEnsamble)  when &OrderedBy = 2 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order PaletNoSerie  when &OrderedBy = 3 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (PaletNoSerie)  when &OrderedBy = 3 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order PaletTipo  when &OrderedBy = 4 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (PaletTipo)  when &OrderedBy = 4 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order PaletEstatus  when &OrderedBy = 5 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (PaletEstatus)  when &OrderedBy = 5 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order PaletProductoNombre  when &OrderedBy = 6 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (PaletProductoNombre)  when &OrderedBy = 6 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order CarreteNoSerie  when &OrderedBy = 7 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (CarreteNoSerie)  when &OrderedBy = 7 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		
		using CarreteEnPalletDS(&FilterFullText, &TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble, &TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble_To, &TFPaletNoSerie, &TFPaletNoSerie_Sel, &TFPaletTipo_Sels
					, &TFPaletEstatus_Sels, &TFPaletProductoNombre, &TFPaletProductoNombre_Sel, &TFCarreteNoSerie, &TFCarreteNoSerie_Sel)
		Where PaletEstatus in (Produccion.EstatusPalet.Embarcado, EstatusPalet.Terminado)
		Where PaletTipo in (TipoPalet.Interno)

		// Write cell values
		&CellRow += 1
		
		Do 'BeforeWriteLine'
		&VisibleColumnCount = 0
		For &ColumnsSelector_Column in &ColumnsSelector.Columns
			If &ColumnsSelector_Column.IsVisible = True
				Do Case
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'PaletHoraFinEnsamble'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Date = DB.PaletHoraFinEnsamble
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'PaletNoSerie'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(PaletNoSerie)
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'PaletTipo'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = PaletTipo.EnumerationDescription()
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'PaletEstatus'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = PaletEstatus.EnumerationDescription()
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'PaletProductoNombre'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(PaletProductoNombre)
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'CarreteNoSerie'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(CarreteNoSerie)
				EndCase
				&VisibleColumnCount += 1
			EndIf
		EndFor		
		
		Do 'AfterWriteLine'

	Endfor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'CloseDocument'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&ExcelDocument.Save()
	Do 'CheckStatus'
	&ExcelDocument.Close()

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'CheckStatus'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	If (&ExcelDocument.ErrCode <> 0)
		&Filename = ""
		&ErrorMessage = &ExcelDocument.ErrDescription
		&ExcelDocument.Close()
		Return
	EndIf

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'InitializeColumnsSelector'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&ColumnsSelector = new WWPColumnsSelector()
	WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"PaletHoraFinEnsamble", '', !'Fecha Terminado (Pallet)', True, '')
	WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"PaletNoSerie", '', !'Pallet', True, '')
	WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"PaletTipo", '', !'Tipo', True, '')
	WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"PaletEstatus", '', !'Estatus', True, '')
	WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"PaletProductoNombre", '', !'Producto', True, '')
	WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"CarreteNoSerie", '', !'Carrete', True, '')
		
	&UserCustomValue = LoadColumnsSelectorState.Udp(!'Reportes.CarreteEnPalletColumnsSelector')
	If not(&UserCustomValue.IsEmpty())
		&ColumnsSelectorAux.FromXml(&UserCustomValue)
		WWP_ColumnSelector_UpdateColumns(&ColumnsSelectorAux, &ColumnsSelector)
	EndIf

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'LoadGridState'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	// Load grid state from session.
	If &Session.Get(!"Reportes.CarreteEnPalletGridState") = !""
		&GridState.FromXml(LoadGridState.Udp(!"Reportes.CarreteEnPalletGridState"))
	Else
		&GridState.FromXml(&Session.Get(!"Reportes.CarreteEnPalletGridState"))
	Endif	

	&OrderedBy = &GridState.OrderedBy
	&OrderedDsc = &GridState.OrderedDsc

	For &GridStateFilterValue in &GridState.FilterValues
		Do Case
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"FILTERFULLTEXT"
				&FilterFullText.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPALETHORAFINENSAMBLE"
				&TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
				&TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble_To.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.ValueTo)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPALETNOSERIE"
				&TFPaletNoSerie.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPALETNOSERIE_SEL"
				&TFPaletNoSerie_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPALETTIPO_SEL"
				&TFPaletTipo_SelsJson = &GridStateFilterValue.Value
				&TFPaletTipo_Sels.FromJson(&TFPaletTipo_SelsJson)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPALETESTATUS_SEL"
				&TFPaletEstatus_SelsJson = &GridStateFilterValue.Value
				&TFPaletEstatus_Sels.FromJson(&TFPaletEstatus_SelsJson)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPALETPRODUCTONOMBRE"
				&TFPaletProductoNombre.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPALETPRODUCTONOMBRE_SEL"
				&TFPaletProductoNombre_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFCARRETENOSERIE"
				&TFCarreteNoSerie.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFCARRETENOSERIE_SEL"
				&TFCarreteNoSerie_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
		EndCase
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'BeforeWriteLine'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */



	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

=== Procedure: Reportes.CarreteEnPalletExportReport ===

--- Source (Source) ---

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

// Exports the contents of a grid (using the selected order and filters) to a PDF file.
&IsAuthorized = WWPBaseObjects.SecGAMIsAuthByFunctionalityKey.Udp(!'carreteenpallet_Execute') 
If &IsAuthorized

	LoadWWPContext.Call(&WWPContext)

Do 'LoadGridState'

&Title = "Palet Carrete List"

//Report Template Source
	Header
		&AppName = "DVelop Software Solutions"
		&Phone = !"+1 550 8923"
		&Mail = !"info@mail.com"
		&Website = !"http://www.web.com"
		&AddressLine1 = !"French Boulevard 2859"
		&AddressLine2 = !"Downtown"
		&AddressLine3 = !"Paris, France"
		Print printTitle
	End 
	Footer
		&PageInfo = "Page: " + &Page.ToString().Trim()
		&DateInfo = "Date: " + &Today.ToFormattedString()
		Print printFooter
	End

	Do 'PrintFilters'

	Do 'PrintColumnTitles'

	Do 'PrintData'

	Do 'PrintFooter'

EndIf

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */


Sub 'PrintFilters'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	If not &FilterFullText.IsEmpty()
		print printBlockFilterFilterFullText
	EndIf
	If not (&TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble.IsEmpty() AND &TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble_To.IsEmpty())
		print printBlockTFPaletHoraFinEnsamble
		&TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble_To_Description = format('%1 (%2)', "Fecha Terminado (Pallet)", "WWP_TSTo")
		print printBlockTFPaletHoraFinEnsamble_To
	EndIf
	If not &TFPaletNoSerie_Sel.IsEmpty()
		print printBlockTFPaletNoSerie_Sel
	Else
		If not &TFPaletNoSerie.IsEmpty()
			print printBlockTFPaletNoSerie
		EndIf
	EndIf
	&TFPaletTipo_Sels.FromJson(&TFPaletTipo_SelsJson)
	If not &TFPaletTipo_Sels.Count = 0
		&i = 1
		For &TFPaletTipo_Sel in &TFPaletTipo_Sels
			If &i = 1
				&TFPaletTipo_SelDscs = ''
			Else
				&TFPaletTipo_SelDscs += ', '
			EndIf
			&FilterTFPaletTipo_SelValueDescription = &TFPaletTipo_Sel.EnumerationDescription()

			&TFPaletTipo_SelDscs += &FilterTFPaletTipo_SelValueDescription
			&i += 1
		EndFor
		print printBlockTFPaletTipo_Sel
	EndIf
	&TFPaletEstatus_Sels.FromJson(&TFPaletEstatus_SelsJson)
	If not &TFPaletEstatus_Sels.Count = 0
		&i = 1
		For &TFPaletEstatus_Sel in &TFPaletEstatus_Sels
			If &i = 1
				&TFPaletEstatus_SelDscs = ''
			Else
				&TFPaletEstatus_SelDscs += ', '
			EndIf
			&FilterTFPaletEstatus_SelValueDescription = &TFPaletEstatus_Sel.EnumerationDescription()

			&TFPaletEstatus_SelDscs += &FilterTFPaletEstatus_SelValueDescription
			&i += 1
		EndFor
		print printBlockTFPaletEstatus_Sel
	EndIf
	If not &TFPaletProductoNombre_Sel.IsEmpty()
		print printBlockTFPaletProductoNombre_Sel
	Else
		If not &TFPaletProductoNombre.IsEmpty()
			print printBlockTFPaletProductoNombre
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If not &TFCarreteNoSerie_Sel.IsEmpty()
		print printBlockTFCarreteNoSerie_Sel
	Else
		If not &TFCarreteNoSerie.IsEmpty()
			print printBlockTFCarreteNoSerie
		EndIf
	EndIf

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'PrintColumnTitles'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	print printBlockBeforeGrid
	print printBlockLines_titles

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'PrintData'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	For each DB.PaletCarrete
		order PaletCarreteId  when &OrderedBy = 1
		order PaletHoraFinEnsamble  when &OrderedBy = 2 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (PaletHoraFinEnsamble)  when &OrderedBy = 2 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order PaletNoSerie  when &OrderedBy = 3 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (PaletNoSerie)  when &OrderedBy = 3 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order PaletTipo  when &OrderedBy = 4 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (PaletTipo)  when &OrderedBy = 4 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order PaletEstatus  when &OrderedBy = 5 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (PaletEstatus)  when &OrderedBy = 5 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order PaletProductoNombre  when &OrderedBy = 6 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (PaletProductoNombre)  when &OrderedBy = 6 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order CarreteNoSerie  when &OrderedBy = 7 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (CarreteNoSerie)  when &OrderedBy = 7 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		
		using CarreteEnPalletDS(&FilterFullText, &TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble, &TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble_To, &TFPaletNoSerie, &TFPaletNoSerie_Sel, &TFPaletTipo_Sels
					, &TFPaletEstatus_Sels, &TFPaletProductoNombre, &TFPaletProductoNombre_Sel, &TFCarreteNoSerie, &TFCarreteNoSerie_Sel)
		Where PaletEstatus in (EstatusPalet.Embarcado, EstatusPalet.Terminado)
		Where PaletTipo in (TipoPalet.Interno)
		&PaletTipoDescription = PaletTipo.EnumerationDescription()
		&PaletEstatusDescription = PaletEstatus.EnumerationDescription()

		Do 'BeforePrintLine'
		print printBlockLines_data
		Do 'AfterPrintLine'
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'LoadGridState'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	// Load grid state from session.
	If &Session.Get(!"Reportes.CarreteEnPalletGridState") = !""
		&GridState.FromXml(LoadGridState.Udp(!"Reportes.CarreteEnPalletGridState"))
	Else
		&GridState.FromXml(&Session.Get(!"Reportes.CarreteEnPalletGridState"))
	Endif	

	&OrderedBy = &GridState.OrderedBy
	&OrderedDsc = &GridState.OrderedDsc

	For &GridStateFilterValue in &GridState.FilterValues
		Do Case
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"FILTERFULLTEXT"
				&FilterFullText.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPALETHORAFINENSAMBLE"
				&TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
				&TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble_To.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.ValueTo)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPALETNOSERIE"
				&TFPaletNoSerie.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPALETNOSERIE_SEL"
				&TFPaletNoSerie_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPALETTIPO_SEL"
				&TFPaletTipo_SelsJson = &GridStateFilterValue.Value
				&TFPaletTipo_Sels.FromJson(&TFPaletTipo_SelsJson)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPALETESTATUS_SEL"
				&TFPaletEstatus_SelsJson = &GridStateFilterValue.Value
				&TFPaletEstatus_Sels.FromJson(&TFPaletEstatus_SelsJson)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPALETPRODUCTONOMBRE"
				&TFPaletProductoNombre.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPALETPRODUCTONOMBRE_SEL"
				&TFPaletProductoNombre_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFCARRETENOSERIE"
				&TFCarreteNoSerie.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFCARRETENOSERIE_SEL"
				&TFCarreteNoSerie_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
		EndCase
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'BeforePrintLine'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */



	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

=== Procedure: Reportes.CarreteEnPalletGetFilterData ===

--- Source (Source) ---

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

&Options = new()
&OptionsDesc = new()
&OptionIndexes = new()
LoadWWPContext.Call(&WWPContext)

Do 'LoadGridState'
Do Case
	Case &DDOName.ToUpper() = !'DDO_PALETNOSERIE'
		Do 'LoadPaletNoSerieOptions'
	Case &DDOName.ToUpper() = !'DDO_PALETPRODUCTONOMBRE'
		Do 'LoadPaletProductoNombreOptions'
	Case &DDOName.ToUpper() = !'DDO_CARRETENOSERIE'
		Do 'LoadCarreteNoSerieOptions'
EndCase

&OptionsJson = &Options.ToJson()
&OptionsDescJson = &OptionsDesc.ToJson()
&OptionIndexesJson = &OptionIndexes.ToJson()

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */


Sub 'LoadGridState'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	// Load grid state from session.
	If &Session.Get(!"Reportes.CarreteEnPalletGridState") = !""
		&GridState.FromXml(LoadGridState.Udp(!"Reportes.CarreteEnPalletGridState"))
	Else
		&GridState.FromXml(&Session.Get(!"Reportes.CarreteEnPalletGridState"))
	Endif	

	For &GridStateFilterValue in &GridState.FilterValues
		Do Case
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"FILTERFULLTEXT"
				&FilterFullText.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPALETHORAFINENSAMBLE"
				&TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
				&TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble_To.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.ValueTo)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPALETNOSERIE"
				&TFPaletNoSerie.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPALETNOSERIE_SEL"
				&TFPaletNoSerie_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPALETTIPO_SEL"
				&TFPaletTipo_SelsJson = &GridStateFilterValue.Value
				&TFPaletTipo_Sels.FromJson(&TFPaletTipo_SelsJson)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPALETESTATUS_SEL"
				&TFPaletEstatus_SelsJson = &GridStateFilterValue.Value
				&TFPaletEstatus_Sels.FromJson(&TFPaletEstatus_SelsJson)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPALETPRODUCTONOMBRE"
				&TFPaletProductoNombre.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPALETPRODUCTONOMBRE_SEL"
				&TFPaletProductoNombre_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFCARRETENOSERIE"
				&TFCarreteNoSerie.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFCARRETENOSERIE_SEL"
				&TFCarreteNoSerie_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
		EndCase
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'LoadPaletNoSerieOptions'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&TFPaletNoSerie = &SearchTxt
	&TFPaletNoSerie_Sel.SetEmpty()
	For Each DB.PaletCarrete
		Order PaletId
		using CarreteEnPalletDS(&FilterFullText, &TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble, &TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble_To, &TFPaletNoSerie, &TFPaletNoSerie_Sel, &TFPaletTipo_Sels
					, &TFPaletEstatus_Sels, &TFPaletProductoNombre, &TFPaletProductoNombre_Sel, &TFCarreteNoSerie, &TFCarreteNoSerie_Sel)
		Where PaletEstatus in (EstatusPalet.Embarcado, EstatusPalet.Terminado)
		Where PaletTipo in (TipoPalet.Interno)

		&count = 0
		For Each DB.PaletCarrete
			Order PaletId
			&count += 1
		EndFor
		If not PaletNoSerie.IsEmpty()
			&Option = PaletNoSerie
			
			&InsertIndex = 1
			Do while &InsertIndex <= &Options.Count AND &Options.Item(&InsertIndex) < &Option
				&InsertIndex = &InsertIndex + 1
			EndDo
			&Options.Add(&Option, &InsertIndex)
			&OptionIndexes.Add(trim(&count.ToFormattedString()), &InsertIndex)
		EndIf
		If &Options.Count = 50
			Exit
		EndIf
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'LoadPaletProductoNombreOptions'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&TFPaletProductoNombre = &SearchTxt
	&TFPaletProductoNombre_Sel.SetEmpty()
	For Each DB.PaletCarrete
		Order PaletProductoNombre
		using CarreteEnPalletDS(&FilterFullText, &TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble, &TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble_To, &TFPaletNoSerie, &TFPaletNoSerie_Sel, &TFPaletTipo_Sels
					, &TFPaletEstatus_Sels, &TFPaletProductoNombre, &TFPaletProductoNombre_Sel, &TFCarreteNoSerie, &TFCarreteNoSerie_Sel)
		Where PaletEstatus in (EstatusPalet.Embarcado, EstatusPalet.Terminado)
		Where PaletTipo in (TipoPalet.Interno)

		&count = 0
		For Each DB.PaletCarrete
			Order PaletProductoNombre
			&count += 1
		EndFor
		If not PaletProductoNombre.IsEmpty()
			&Option = PaletProductoNombre
			
			&Options.Add(&Option)
			&OptionIndexes.Add(trim(&count.ToFormattedString()))
		EndIf
		If &Options.Count = 50
			Exit
		EndIf
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'LoadCarreteNoSerieOptions'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&TFCarreteNoSerie = &SearchTxt
	&TFCarreteNoSerie_Sel.SetEmpty()
	For Each DB.PaletCarrete
		Order CarreteNoSerie
		using CarreteEnPalletDS(&FilterFullText, &TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble, &TFPaletHoraFinEnsamble_To, &TFPaletNoSerie, &TFPaletNoSerie_Sel, &TFPaletTipo_Sels
					, &TFPaletEstatus_Sels, &TFPaletProductoNombre, &TFPaletProductoNombre_Sel, &TFCarreteNoSerie, &TFCarreteNoSerie_Sel)
		Where PaletEstatus in (EstatusPalet.Embarcado, EstatusPalet.Terminado)
		Where PaletTipo in (TipoPalet.Interno)

		&count = 0
		For Each DB.PaletCarrete
			Order CarreteNoSerie
			&count += 1
		EndFor
		If not CarreteNoSerie.IsEmpty()
			&Option = CarreteNoSerie
			
			&Options.Add(&Option)
			&OptionIndexes.Add(trim(&count.ToFormattedString()))
		EndIf
		If &Options.Count = 50
			Exit
		EndIf
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

=== Procedure: Reportes.CausaInterrupcionWWExport ===

--- Source (Source) ---

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

// Exports the contents of a grid (using the selected order and filters) to an Excel file.
LoadWWPContext.Call(&WWPContext)

Do 'OpenDocument'

&CellRow = 1
&FirstColumn = 1

Do 'LoadGridState'

Do 'WriteFilters'

Do 'WriteColumnTitles'

Do 'WriteData'

Do 'CloseDocument'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */


Sub 'OpenDocument'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&Random = Random() * 10000
	&Filename = !"CausaInterrupcionWWExport-" + &Random.ToString().Trim() + !".xlsx"

	&ExcelDocument.Open(&Filename)
	Do 'CheckStatus'
	&ExcelDocument.Clear()

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'WriteFilters'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	If not (&FilterFullText.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "WWP_FullTextFilterDescription")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&FilterFullText)
	EndIf
	If not (&TFCausaInterrupcionNombre_Sel.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Nombre")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFCausaInterrupcionNombre_Sel)
	Else
		If not (&TFCausaInterrupcionNombre.IsEmpty())
			WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Nombre")
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFCausaInterrupcionNombre)
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If not (&TFCausaInterrupcionPrensa_Sel.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Prensa")
		Do Case
			Case &TFCausaInterrupcionPrensa_Sel = 1
				&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = "WWP_TSChecked"
			Case &TFCausaInterrupcionPrensa_Sel = 2
				&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = "WWP_TSUnChecked"
		EndCase
	EndIf
	If not (&TFCausaInterrupcionExtrusora_Sel.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Extrusora")
		Do Case
			Case &TFCausaInterrupcionExtrusora_Sel = 1
				&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = "WWP_TSChecked"
			Case &TFCausaInterrupcionExtrusora_Sel = 2
				&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = "WWP_TSUnChecked"
		EndCase
	EndIf
	&CellRow += 2

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'WriteColumnTitles'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&VisibleColumnCount = 0
	If &Session.Get(!'Reportes.CausaInterrupcionWWColumnsSelector') <> ''
		&ColumnsSelectorXML = &Session.Get(!'Reportes.CausaInterrupcionWWColumnsSelector')
		&ColumnsSelector.FromXml(&ColumnsSelectorXML)
	Else
		Do 'InitializeColumnsSelector'
	EndIf

	&ColumnsSelector.Columns.Sort(!'Order')
	For &ColumnsSelector_Column in &ColumnsSelector.Columns
		If &ColumnsSelector_Column.IsVisible = True
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = GetMessageText(iif(&ColumnsSelector_Column.DisplayName.IsEmpty(), &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName, &ColumnsSelector_Column.DisplayName))
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Bold = True
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Color = 11
			&VisibleColumnCount += 1
		EndIf
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'WriteData'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	For each CausaInterrupcion
		order CausaInterrupcionNombre  when &OrderedBy = 1 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (CausaInterrupcionNombre)  when &OrderedBy = 1 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order CausaInterrupcionPrensa  when &OrderedBy = 2 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (CausaInterrupcionPrensa)  when &OrderedBy = 2 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order CausaInterrupcionExtrusora  when &OrderedBy = 3 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (CausaInterrupcionExtrusora)  when &OrderedBy = 3 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		
		using CausaInterrupcionWWDS(&FilterFullText, &TFCausaInterrupcionNombre, &TFCausaInterrupcionNombre_Sel, &TFCausaInterrupcionPrensa_Sel, &TFCausaInterrupcionExtrusora_Sel)

		// Write cell values
		&CellRow += 1
		
		Do 'BeforeWriteLine'
		&VisibleColumnCount = 0
		For &ColumnsSelector_Column in &ColumnsSelector.Columns
			If &ColumnsSelector_Column.IsVisible = True
				Do Case
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'CausaInterrupcionNombre'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(CausaInterrupcionNombre)
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'CausaInterrupcionPrensa'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = CausaInterrupcionPrensa.ToString()
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'CausaInterrupcionExtrusora'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = CausaInterrupcionExtrusora.ToString()
				EndCase
				&VisibleColumnCount += 1
			EndIf
		EndFor		
		
		Do 'AfterWriteLine'

	Endfor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'CloseDocument'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&ExcelDocument.Save()
	Do 'CheckStatus'
	&ExcelDocument.Close()

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'CheckStatus'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	If (&ExcelDocument.ErrCode <> 0)
		&Filename = ""
		&ErrorMessage = &ExcelDocument.ErrDescription
		&ExcelDocument.Close()
		Return
	EndIf

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'InitializeColumnsSelector'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&ColumnsSelector = new WWPColumnsSelector()
	WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"CausaInterrupcionNombre", '', !'Nombre', True, '')
	WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"CausaInterrupcionPrensa", '', !'Prensa', True, '')
	WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"CausaInterrupcionExtrusora", '', !'Extrusora', True, '')
		
	&UserCustomValue = LoadColumnsSelectorState.Udp(!'Reportes.CausaInterrupcionWWColumnsSelector')
	If not(&UserCustomValue.IsEmpty())
		&ColumnsSelectorAux.FromXml(&UserCustomValue)
		WWP_ColumnSelector_UpdateColumns(&ColumnsSelectorAux, &ColumnsSelector)
	EndIf

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'LoadGridState'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	// Load grid state from session.
	If &Session.Get(!"Reportes.CausaInterrupcionWWGridState") = !""
		&GridState.FromXml(LoadGridState.Udp(!"Reportes.CausaInterrupcionWWGridState"))
	Else
		&GridState.FromXml(&Session.Get(!"Reportes.CausaInterrupcionWWGridState"))
	Endif	

	&OrderedBy = &GridState.OrderedBy
	&OrderedDsc = &GridState.OrderedDsc

	For &GridStateFilterValue in &GridState.FilterValues
		Do Case
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"FILTERFULLTEXT"
				&FilterFullText.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFCAUSAINTERRUPCIONNOMBRE"
				&TFCausaInterrupcionNombre.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFCAUSAINTERRUPCIONNOMBRE_SEL"
				&TFCausaInterrupcionNombre_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFCAUSAINTERRUPCIONPRENSA_SEL"
				&TFCausaInterrupcionPrensa_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFCAUSAINTERRUPCIONEXTRUSORA_SEL"
				&TFCausaInterrupcionExtrusora_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
		EndCase
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub
