=== Procedure: Reportes.CausaInterrupcionWWGetFilterData ===

--- Source (Source) ---

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

&Options = new()
&OptionsDesc = new()
&OptionIndexes = new()
LoadWWPContext.Call(&WWPContext)

Do 'LoadGridState'
Do Case
	Case &DDOName.ToUpper() = !'DDO_CAUSAINTERRUPCIONNOMBRE'
		Do 'LoadCausaInterrupcionNombreOptions'
EndCase

&OptionsJson = &Options.ToJson()
&OptionsDescJson = &OptionsDesc.ToJson()
&OptionIndexesJson = &OptionIndexes.ToJson()

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */


Sub 'LoadGridState'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	// Load grid state from session.
	If &Session.Get(!"Reportes.CausaInterrupcionWWGridState") = !""
		&GridState.FromXml(LoadGridState.Udp(!"Reportes.CausaInterrupcionWWGridState"))
	Else
		&GridState.FromXml(&Session.Get(!"Reportes.CausaInterrupcionWWGridState"))
	Endif	

	For &GridStateFilterValue in &GridState.FilterValues
		Do Case
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"FILTERFULLTEXT"
				&FilterFullText.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFCAUSAINTERRUPCIONNOMBRE"
				&TFCausaInterrupcionNombre.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFCAUSAINTERRUPCIONNOMBRE_SEL"
				&TFCausaInterrupcionNombre_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFCAUSAINTERRUPCIONPRENSA_SEL"
				&TFCausaInterrupcionPrensa_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFCAUSAINTERRUPCIONEXTRUSORA_SEL"
				&TFCausaInterrupcionExtrusora_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
		EndCase
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'LoadCausaInterrupcionNombreOptions'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&TFCausaInterrupcionNombre = &SearchTxt
	&TFCausaInterrupcionNombre_Sel.SetEmpty()
	For Each CausaInterrupcion
		Order CausaInterrupcionNombre
		using CausaInterrupcionWWDS(&FilterFullText, &TFCausaInterrupcionNombre, &TFCausaInterrupcionNombre_Sel, &TFCausaInterrupcionPrensa_Sel, &TFCausaInterrupcionExtrusora_Sel)

		&count = 0
		For Each CausaInterrupcion
			Order CausaInterrupcionNombre
			&count += 1
		EndFor
		If not CausaInterrupcionNombre.IsEmpty()
			&Option = CausaInterrupcionNombre
			
			&Options.Add(&Option)
			&OptionIndexes.Add(trim(&count.ToFormattedString()))
		EndIf
		If &Options.Count = 50
			Exit
		EndIf
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

--- Rules (Rules) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

=== Transaction: Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacion ===

--- Start (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	LoadWWPContext.Call(&WWPContext)
	&TrnContext.FromXml(&WebSession.Get(!"TrnContext"))
	If (&TrnContext.TransactionName = &Pgmname and &Mode = TrnMode.Insert)
		For &TrnContextAtt in &TrnContext.Attributes
			Do Case
				// When inserting with instantiated TurnoId
				Case &TrnContextAtt.AttributeName = !"TurnoId"
					&Insert_TurnoId.FromString(&TrnContextAtt.AttributeValue)
				// When inserting with instantiated ExtrusoraId
				Case &TrnContextAtt.AttributeName = !"ExtrusoraId"
					&Insert_ExtrusoraId.FromString(&TrnContextAtt.AttributeValue)
			Endcase
		Endfor
	Endif
	
    [web]
    {
	ExtrusoraObservacionId.Visible = False
	}
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- After Trn (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	AuditTransaction.Call(&AuditingObject,&pgmname)	
   [web]
   {
	
	If (&Mode = TrnMode.Delete and not &TrnContext.CallerOnDelete)
		ExtrusoraObservacionWW.Call()
	Endif
	Return
   }
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- Rules (Rules) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
[web]
{
parm(in:&Mode, in:&ExtrusoraObservacionId);

ExtrusoraObservacionId = &ExtrusoraObservacionId if not &ExtrusoraObservacionId.IsEmpty();
noaccept(ExtrusoraObservacionId);
noprompt(ExtrusoraObservacionId);

TurnoId = &Insert_TurnoId if &Mode = TrnMode.Insert and not &Insert_TurnoId.IsEmpty();
noaccept(TurnoId) if &Mode = TrnMode.Insert and not &Insert_TurnoId.IsEmpty();
ExtrusoraId = &Insert_ExtrusoraId if &Mode = TrnMode.Insert and not &Insert_ExtrusoraId.IsEmpty();
noaccept(ExtrusoraId) if &Mode = TrnMode.Insert and not &Insert_ExtrusoraId.IsEmpty();
}

LoadAuditExtrusoraObservacion.Call(!'Y', &AuditingObject, ExtrusoraObservacionId, &Mode) on beforeUpdate;
LoadAuditExtrusoraObservacion.Call(!'Y', &AuditingObject, ExtrusoraObservacionId, &Mode) on beforeDelete;
LoadAuditExtrusoraObservacion.Call(!'N', &AuditingObject, ExtrusoraObservacionId, &Mode) If Insert on BeforeComplete;
LoadAuditExtrusoraObservacion.Call(!'N', &AuditingObject, ExtrusoraObservacionId, &Mode) If Update on BeforeComplete;

=== Procedure: Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacionWWExport ===

--- Source (Source) ---

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

// Exports the contents of a grid (using the selected order and filters) to an Excel file.
LoadWWPContext.Call(&WWPContext)

Do 'OpenDocument'

&CellRow = 1
&FirstColumn = 1

Do 'LoadGridState'

Do 'WriteFilters'

Do 'WriteColumnTitles'

Do 'WriteData'

Do 'CloseDocument'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */


Sub 'OpenDocument'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&Random = Random() * 10000
	&Filename = !"ExtrusoraObservacionWWExport-" + &Random.ToString().Trim() + !".xlsx"

	&ExcelDocument.Open(&Filename)
	Do 'CheckStatus'
	&ExcelDocument.Clear()

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'WriteFilters'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	If not (&FilterFullText.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "WWP_FullTextFilterDescription")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&FilterFullText)
	EndIf
	If not (&TFExtrusoraObservacionFecha.IsEmpty() AND &TFExtrusoraObservacionFecha_To.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Fecha")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Date = &TFExtrusoraObservacionFecha
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, false, &CellRow, &FirstColumn + 2, "WWP_TSTo")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 3).Date = &TFExtrusoraObservacionFecha_To
	EndIf
	If not (&TFExtrusoraNombre_Sel.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Extrusora")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWPBaseObjects.WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFExtrusoraNombre_Sel)
	Else
		If not (&TFExtrusoraNombre.IsEmpty())
			WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Extrusora")
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFExtrusoraNombre)
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If not (&TFTurnoNombre_Sel.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Turno")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFTurnoNombre_Sel)
	Else
		If not (&TFTurnoNombre.IsEmpty())
			WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Turno")
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFTurnoNombre)
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If not (&TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion.IsEmpty() AND &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To.IsEmpty())
		WWPBaseObjects.WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Interrupción (Hr)")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Number = &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, false, &CellRow, &FirstColumn + 2, "WWP_TSTo")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 3).Number = &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To
	EndIf
	If not (&TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor.IsEmpty() AND &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Tiempo (Hr)")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Number = &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, false, &CellRow, &FirstColumn + 2, "WWP_TSTo")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 3).Number = &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To
	EndIf
	If not (&TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion_Sel.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Descripción")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion_Sel)
	Else
		If not (&TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion.IsEmpty())
			WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Descripción")
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion)
		EndIf
	EndIf
	&CellRow += 2

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'WriteColumnTitles'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&VisibleColumnCount = 0
	If &Session.Get(!'Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacionWWColumnsSelector') <> ''
		&ColumnsSelectorXML = &Session.Get(!'Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacionWWColumnsSelector')
		&ColumnsSelector.FromXml(&ColumnsSelectorXML)
	Else
		Do 'InitializeColumnsSelector'
	EndIf

	&ColumnsSelector.Columns.Sort(!'Order')
	For &ColumnsSelector_Column in &ColumnsSelector.Columns
		If &ColumnsSelector_Column.IsVisible = True
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = GetMessageText(iif(&ColumnsSelector_Column.DisplayName.IsEmpty(), &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName, &ColumnsSelector_Column.DisplayName))
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Bold = True
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Color = 11
			&VisibleColumnCount += 1
		EndIf
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'WriteData'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	For each ExtrusoraObservacion
		order ExtrusoraObservacionFecha  when &OrderedBy = 1 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (ExtrusoraObservacionFecha)  when &OrderedBy = 1 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order ExtrusoraNombre  when &OrderedBy = 2 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (ExtrusoraNombre)  when &OrderedBy = 2 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order TurnoNombre  when &OrderedBy = 3 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (TurnoNombre)  when &OrderedBy = 3 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order ExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion  when &OrderedBy = 4 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (ExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion)  when &OrderedBy = 4 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order ExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor  when &OrderedBy = 5 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (ExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor)  when &OrderedBy = 5 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order ExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion  when &OrderedBy = 6 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (ExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion)  when &OrderedBy = 6 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		
		using Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacionWWDS(&FilterFullText, &TFExtrusoraObservacionFecha, &TFExtrusoraObservacionFecha_To, &TFExtrusoraNombre, &TFExtrusoraNombre_Sel, &TFTurnoNombre
					, &TFTurnoNombre_Sel, &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion, &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To, &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor, &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To, &TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion
					, &TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion_Sel)

		// Write cell values
		&CellRow += 1
		
		Do 'BeforeWriteLine'
		&VisibleColumnCount = 0
		For &ColumnsSelector_Column in &ColumnsSelector.Columns
			If &ColumnsSelector_Column.IsVisible = True
				Do Case
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'ExtrusoraObservacionFecha'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Date = ExtrusoraObservacionFecha
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'ExtrusoraNombre'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(ExtrusoraNombre)
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'TurnoNombre'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(TurnoNombre)
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'ExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Number = ExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'ExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Number = ExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'ExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(ExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion)
				EndCase
				&VisibleColumnCount += 1
			EndIf
		EndFor		
		
		Do 'AfterWriteLine'

	Endfor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'CloseDocument'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&ExcelDocument.Save()
	Do 'CheckStatus'
	&ExcelDocument.Close()

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'CheckStatus'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	If (&ExcelDocument.ErrCode <> 0)
		&Filename = ""
		&ErrorMessage = &ExcelDocument.ErrDescription
		&ExcelDocument.Close()
		Return
	EndIf

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'InitializeColumnsSelector'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&ColumnsSelector = new WWPColumnsSelector()
	WWPBaseObjects.WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"ExtrusoraObservacionFecha", '', !'Fecha', True, '')
	WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"ExtrusoraNombre", '', !'Extrusora', True, '')
	WWPBaseObjects.WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"TurnoNombre", '', !'Turno', True, '')
	WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"ExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion", '', !'Interrupción (Hr)', True, '')
	WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"ExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor", '', !'Tiempo (Hr)', True, '')
	WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"ExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion", '', !'Descripción', True, '')
		
	&UserCustomValue = LoadColumnsSelectorState.Udp(!'Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacionWWColumnsSelector')
	If not(&UserCustomValue.IsEmpty())
		&ColumnsSelectorAux.FromXml(&UserCustomValue)
		WWP_ColumnSelector_UpdateColumns(&ColumnsSelectorAux, &ColumnsSelector)
	EndIf

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'LoadGridState'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	// Load grid state from session.
	If &Session.Get(!"Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacionWWGridState") = !""
		&GridState.FromXml(LoadGridState.Udp(!"Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacionWWGridState"))
	Else
		&GridState.FromXml(&Session.Get(!"Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacionWWGridState"))
	Endif	

	&OrderedBy = &GridState.OrderedBy
	&OrderedDsc = &GridState.OrderedDsc

	For &GridStateFilterValue in &GridState.FilterValues
		Do Case
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"FILTERFULLTEXT"
				&FilterFullText.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEXTRUSORAOBSERVACIONFECHA"
				&TFExtrusoraObservacionFecha.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
				&TFExtrusoraObservacionFecha_To.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.ValueTo)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEXTRUSORANOMBRE"
				&TFExtrusoraNombre.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEXTRUSORANOMBRE_SEL"
				&TFExtrusoraNombre_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFTURNONOMBRE"
				&TFTurnoNombre.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFTURNONOMBRE_SEL"
				&TFTurnoNombre_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEXTRUSORAOBSERVACIONTIEMPOINTERRUPCION"
				&TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
				&TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.ValueTo)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEXTRUSORAOBSERVACIONTIEMPOSUPERVISOR"
				&TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
				&TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.ValueTo)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEXTRUSORAOBSERVACIONDESCRIPCION"
				&TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEXTRUSORAOBSERVACIONDESCRIPCION_SEL"
				&TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
		EndCase
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

=== Procedure: Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacionWWExportReport ===

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

// Exports the contents of a grid (using the selected order and filters) to a PDF file.
&IsAuthorized = SecGAMIsAuthByFunctionalityKey.Udp(!'extrusoraobservacionww_Execute') 
If &IsAuthorized

	LoadWWPContext.Call(&WWPContext)

Do 'LoadGridState'

&Title = "Extrusora Observacion List"

//Report Template Source
	Header
		&AppName = "DVelop Software Solutions"
		&Phone = !"+1 550 8923"
		&Mail = !"info@mail.com"
		&Website = !"http://www.web.com"
		&AddressLine1 = !"French Boulevard 2859"
		&AddressLine2 = !"Downtown"
		&AddressLine3 = !"Paris, France"
		Print printTitle
	End 
	Footer
		&PageInfo = "Page: " + &Page.ToString().Trim()
		&DateInfo = "Date: " + &Today.ToFormattedString()
		Print printFooter
	End

	Do 'PrintFilters'

	Do 'PrintColumnTitles'

	Do 'PrintData'

	Do 'PrintFooter'

EndIf

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */


Sub 'PrintFilters'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	If not &FilterFullText.IsEmpty()
		print printBlockFilterFilterFullText
	EndIf
	If not (&TFExtrusoraObservacionFecha.IsEmpty() AND &TFExtrusoraObservacionFecha_To.IsEmpty())
		print printBlockTFExtrusoraObservacionFecha
		&TFExtrusoraObservacionFecha_To_Description = format('%1 (%2)', "Fecha", "WWP_TSTo")
		print printBlockTFExtrusoraObservacionFecha_To
	EndIf
	If not &TFExtrusoraNombre_Sel.IsEmpty()
		print printBlockTFExtrusoraNombre_Sel
	Else
		If not &TFExtrusoraNombre.IsEmpty()
			print printBlockTFExtrusoraNombre
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If not &TFTurnoNombre_Sel.IsEmpty()
		print printBlockTFTurnoNombre_Sel
	Else
		If not &TFTurnoNombre.IsEmpty()
			print printBlockTFTurnoNombre
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If not (&TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion.IsEmpty() AND &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To.IsEmpty())
		print printBlockTFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion
		&TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To_Description = format('%1 (%2)', "Interrupción (Hr)", "WWP_TSTo")
		print printBlockTFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To
	EndIf
	If not (&TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor.IsEmpty() AND &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To.IsEmpty())
		print printBlockTFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor
		&TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To_Description = format('%1 (%2)', "Tiempo (Hr)", "WWP_TSTo")
		print printBlockTFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To
	EndIf
	If not &TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion_Sel.IsEmpty()
		print printBlockTFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion_Sel
	Else
		If not &TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion.IsEmpty()
			print printBlockTFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion
		EndIf
	EndIf

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	print printBlockLines_titles

		order (ExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion)  when &OrderedBy = 4 AND &OrderedDsc = True

					, &TFTurnoNombre_Sel, &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion, &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To, &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor, &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To, &TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion
					, &TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion_Sel)

=== Procedure: Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacionWWGetFilterData ===

--- Source (Source) ---

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

&Options = new()
&OptionsDesc = new()
&OptionIndexes = new()
LoadWWPContext.Call(&WWPContext)

Do 'LoadGridState'
Do Case
	Case &DDOName.ToUpper() = !'DDO_EXTRUSORANOMBRE'
		Do 'LoadExtrusoraNombreOptions'
	Case &DDOName.ToUpper() = !'DDO_TURNONOMBRE'
		Do 'LoadTurnoNombreOptions'
	Case &DDOName.ToUpper() = !'DDO_EXTRUSORAOBSERVACIONDESCRIPCION'
		Do 'LoadExtrusoraObservacionDescripcionOptions'
EndCase

&OptionsJson = &Options.ToJson()
&OptionsDescJson = &OptionsDesc.ToJson()
&OptionIndexesJson = &OptionIndexes.ToJson()

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */


Sub 'LoadGridState'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	// Load grid state from session.
	If &Session.Get(!"Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacionWWGridState") = !""
		&GridState.FromXml(LoadGridState.Udp(!"Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacionWWGridState"))
	Else
		&GridState.FromXml(&Session.Get(!"Reportes.ExtrusoraObservacionWWGridState"))
	Endif	

	For &GridStateFilterValue in &GridState.FilterValues
		Do Case
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"FILTERFULLTEXT"
				&FilterFullText.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEXTRUSORAOBSERVACIONFECHA"
				&TFExtrusoraObservacionFecha.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
				&TFExtrusoraObservacionFecha_To.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.ValueTo)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEXTRUSORANOMBRE"
				&TFExtrusoraNombre.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEXTRUSORANOMBRE_SEL"
				&TFExtrusoraNombre_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFTURNONOMBRE"
				&TFTurnoNombre.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFTURNONOMBRE_SEL"
				&TFTurnoNombre_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEXTRUSORAOBSERVACIONTIEMPOINTERRUPCION"
				&TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
				&TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.ValueTo)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEXTRUSORAOBSERVACIONTIEMPOSUPERVISOR"
				&TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
				&TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.ValueTo)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEXTRUSORAOBSERVACIONDESCRIPCION"
				&TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEXTRUSORAOBSERVACIONDESCRIPCION_SEL"
				&TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
		EndCase
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'LoadExtrusoraNombreOptions'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&TFExtrusoraNombre = &SearchTxt
	&TFExtrusoraNombre_Sel.SetEmpty()
	For Each ExtrusoraObservacion
		Order ExtrusoraId
		using ExtrusoraObservacionWWDS(&FilterFullText, &TFExtrusoraObservacionFecha, &TFExtrusoraObservacionFecha_To, &TFExtrusoraNombre, &TFExtrusoraNombre_Sel, &TFTurnoNombre
					, &TFTurnoNombre_Sel, &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion, &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To, &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor, &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To, &TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion
					, &TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion_Sel)

		&count = 0
		For Each ExtrusoraObservacion
			Order ExtrusoraId
			&count += 1
		EndFor
		If not ExtrusoraNombre.IsEmpty()
			&Option = ExtrusoraNombre
			
			&InsertIndex = 1
			Do while &InsertIndex <= &Options.Count AND &Options.Item(&InsertIndex) < &Option
				&InsertIndex = &InsertIndex + 1
			EndDo
			&Options.Add(&Option, &InsertIndex)
			&OptionIndexes.Add(trim(&count.ToFormattedString()), &InsertIndex)
		EndIf
		If &Options.Count = 50
			Exit
		EndIf
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'LoadTurnoNombreOptions'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&TFTurnoNombre = &SearchTxt
	&TFTurnoNombre_Sel.SetEmpty()
	For Each ExtrusoraObservacion
		Order TurnoId
		using ExtrusoraObservacionWWDS(&FilterFullText, &TFExtrusoraObservacionFecha, &TFExtrusoraObservacionFecha_To, &TFExtrusoraNombre, &TFExtrusoraNombre_Sel, &TFTurnoNombre
					, &TFTurnoNombre_Sel, &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion, &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To, &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor, &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To, &TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion
					, &TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion_Sel)

		&count = 0
		For Each ExtrusoraObservacion
			Order TurnoId
			&count += 1
		EndFor
		If not TurnoNombre.IsEmpty()
			&Option = TurnoNombre
			
			&InsertIndex = 1
			Do while &InsertIndex <= &Options.Count AND &Options.Item(&InsertIndex) < &Option
				&InsertIndex = &InsertIndex + 1
			EndDo
			&Options.Add(&Option, &InsertIndex)
			&OptionIndexes.Add(trim(&count.ToFormattedString()), &InsertIndex)
		EndIf
		If &Options.Count = 50
			Exit
		EndIf
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'LoadExtrusoraObservacionDescripcionOptions'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion = &SearchTxt
	&TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion_Sel.SetEmpty()
	For Each ExtrusoraObservacion
		Order ExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion
		using ExtrusoraObservacionWWDS(&FilterFullText, &TFExtrusoraObservacionFecha, &TFExtrusoraObservacionFecha_To, &TFExtrusoraNombre, &TFExtrusoraNombre_Sel, &TFTurnoNombre
					, &TFTurnoNombre_Sel, &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion, &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To, &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor, &TFExtrusoraObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To, &TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion
					, &TFExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion_Sel)

		&count = 0
		For Each ExtrusoraObservacion
			Order ExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion
			&count += 1
		EndFor
		If not ExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion.IsEmpty()
			&Option = ExtrusoraObservacionDescripcion
			
			&Options.Add(&Option)
			&OptionIndexes.Add(trim(&count.ToFormattedString()))
		EndIf
		If &Options.Count = 50
			Exit
		EndIf
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

--- Rules (Rules) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

parm(in:&DDOName, in:&SearchTxt, in:&SearchTxtTo, out:&OptionsJson, out:&OptionsDescJson, out:&OptionIndexesJson);

=== Procedure: Reportes.PalletEnEmbarqueExport ===

--- Source (Source) ---

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

// Exports the contents of a grid (using the selected order and filters) to an Excel file.
LoadWWPContext.Call(&WWPContext)

Do 'OpenDocument'

&CellRow = 1
&FirstColumn = 1

Do 'LoadGridState'

Do 'WriteFilters'

Do 'WriteColumnTitles'

Do 'WriteData'

Do 'CloseDocument'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */


Sub 'OpenDocument'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&Random = Random() * 10000
	&Filename = !"PalletEnEmbarqueExport-" + &Random.ToString().Trim() + !".xlsx"

	&ExcelDocument.Open(&Filename)
	Do 'CheckStatus'
	&ExcelDocument.Clear()

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'WriteFilters'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	If not (&FilterFullText.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "WWP_FullTextFilterDescription")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&FilterFullText)
	EndIf
	If not (&TFEmbarqueFecha.IsEmpty() AND &TFEmbarqueFecha_To.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Fecha")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Date = &TFEmbarqueFecha
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, false, &CellRow, &FirstColumn + 2, "WWP_TSTo")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 3).Date = &TFEmbarqueFecha_To
	EndIf
	If not (&TFEmbarqueCodigo_Sel.IsEmpty())
		WWPBaseObjects.WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Código")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFEmbarqueCodigo_Sel)
	Else
		If not (&TFEmbarqueCodigo.IsEmpty())
			WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Código")
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFEmbarqueCodigo)
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If not (&TFEmbarqueCliente_Sel.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Cliente")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFEmbarqueCliente_Sel)
	Else
		If not (&TFEmbarqueCliente.IsEmpty())
			WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Cliente")
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFEmbarqueCliente)
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If not (&TFEmbarqueClienteGrupo_Sel.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Grupo")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFEmbarqueClienteGrupo_Sel)
	Else
		If not (&TFEmbarqueClienteGrupo.IsEmpty())
			WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Grupo")
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFEmbarqueClienteGrupo)
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If not (&TFEmbarqueClienteEnvia_Sel.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Envío")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFEmbarqueClienteEnvia_Sel)
	Else
		If not (&TFEmbarqueClienteEnvia.IsEmpty())
			WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Envío")
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFEmbarqueClienteEnvia)
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If not (&TFEmbarqueDetalleProducto_Sel.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Producto")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFEmbarqueDetalleProducto_Sel)
	Else
		If not (&TFEmbarqueDetalleProducto.IsEmpty())
			WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Producto")
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFEmbarqueDetalleProducto)
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If not (&TFEmbarquePalletNoPallet_Sel.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Pallet")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWPBaseObjects.WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFEmbarquePalletNoPallet_Sel)
	Else
		If not (&TFEmbarquePalletNoPallet.IsEmpty())
			WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Pallet")
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFEmbarquePalletNoPallet)
		EndIf
	EndIf
	&CellRow += 2

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'WriteColumnTitles'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&VisibleColumnCount = 0
	If &Session.Get(!'Reportes.PalletEnEmbarqueColumnsSelector') <> ''
		&ColumnsSelectorXML = &Session.Get(!'Reportes.PalletEnEmbarqueColumnsSelector')
		&ColumnsSelector.FromXml(&ColumnsSelectorXML)
	Else
		Do 'InitializeColumnsSelector'
	EndIf

	&ColumnsSelector.Columns.Sort(!'Order')
	For &ColumnsSelector_Column in &ColumnsSelector.Columns
		If &ColumnsSelector_Column.IsVisible = True
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = GetMessageText(iif(&ColumnsSelector_Column.DisplayName.IsEmpty(), &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName, &ColumnsSelector_Column.DisplayName))
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Bold = True
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Color = 11
			&VisibleColumnCount += 1
		EndIf
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'WriteData'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	For each DB.EmbarquePallet
		order EmbarquePalletValido  when &OrderedBy = 1
		order EmbarqueFecha  when &OrderedBy = 2 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (EmbarqueFecha)  when &OrderedBy = 2 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order EmbarqueCliente  when &OrderedBy = 3 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (EmbarqueCliente)  when &OrderedBy = 3 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order EmbarqueClienteGrupo  when &OrderedBy = 4 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (EmbarqueClienteGrupo)  when &OrderedBy = 4 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order EmbarqueClienteEnvia  when &OrderedBy = 5 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (EmbarqueClienteEnvia)  when &OrderedBy = 5 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order EmbarqueDetalleProducto  when &OrderedBy = 6 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (EmbarqueDetalleProducto)  when &OrderedBy = 6 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order DB.EmbarquePalletNoPallet  when &OrderedBy = 7 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (EmbarquePalletNoPallet)  when &OrderedBy = 7 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		
		using PalletEnEmbarqueDS(&FilterFullText, &TFEmbarqueFecha, &TFEmbarqueFecha_To, &TFEmbarqueCodigo, &TFEmbarqueCodigo_Sel, &TFEmbarqueCliente
					, &TFEmbarqueCliente_Sel, &TFEmbarqueClienteGrupo, &TFEmbarqueClienteGrupo_Sel, &TFEmbarqueClienteEnvia, &TFEmbarqueClienteEnvia_Sel, &TFEmbarqueDetalleProducto
					, &TFEmbarqueDetalleProducto_Sel, &TFEmbarquePalletNoPallet, &TFEmbarquePalletNoPallet_Sel)
		Where EmbarqueEstatus in (EstatusEmbarque.Finalizado)

		// Write cell values
		&CellRow += 1
		
		Do 'BeforeWriteLine'
		&VisibleColumnCount = 0
		For &ColumnsSelector_Column in &ColumnsSelector.Columns
			If &ColumnsSelector_Column.IsVisible = True
				Do Case
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'EmbarqueFecha'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Date = EmbarqueFecha
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'EmbarqueCodigo'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(EmbarqueCodigo)
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'EmbarqueCliente'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(EmbarqueCliente)
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'EmbarqueClienteGrupo'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(EmbarqueClienteGrupo)
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'EmbarqueClienteEnvia'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(DB.EmbarqueClienteEnvia)
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'EmbarqueDetalleProducto'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(EmbarqueDetalleProducto)
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'EmbarquePalletNoPallet'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(EmbarquePalletNoPallet)
				EndCase
				&VisibleColumnCount += 1
			EndIf
		EndFor		
		
		Do 'AfterWriteLine'

	Endfor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'CloseDocument'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&ExcelDocument.Save()
	Do 'CheckStatus'
	&ExcelDocument.Close()

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'CheckStatus'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	If (&ExcelDocument.ErrCode <> 0)
		&Filename = ""
		&ErrorMessage = &ExcelDocument.ErrDescription
		&ExcelDocument.Close()
		Return
	EndIf

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'InitializeColumnsSelector'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&ColumnsSelector = new WWPColumnsSelector()
	WWPBaseObjects.WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"EmbarqueFecha", '', !'Fecha', True, '')
	WWPBaseObjects.WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"EmbarqueCodigo", '', !'Código', True, '')
	WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"EmbarqueCliente", '', !'Cliente', True, '')
	WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"EmbarqueClienteGrupo", '', !'Grupo', True, '')
	WWPBaseObjects.WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"EmbarqueClienteEnvia", '', !'Envío', True, '')
	WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"EmbarqueDetalleProducto", '', !'Producto', True, '')
	WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"EmbarquePalletNoPallet", '', !'Pallet', True, '')
		
	&UserCustomValue = LoadColumnsSelectorState.Udp(!'Reportes.PalletEnEmbarqueColumnsSelector')
	If not(&UserCustomValue.IsEmpty())
		&ColumnsSelectorAux.FromXml(&UserCustomValue)
		WWP_ColumnSelector_UpdateColumns(&ColumnsSelectorAux, &ColumnsSelector)
	EndIf

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'LoadGridState'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	// Load grid state from session.
	If &Session.Get(!"Reportes.PalletEnEmbarqueGridState") = !""
		&GridState.FromXml(LoadGridState.Udp(!"Reportes.PalletEnEmbarqueGridState"))
	Else
		&GridState.FromXml(&Session.Get(!"Reportes.PalletEnEmbarqueGridState"))
	Endif	

	&OrderedBy = &GridState.OrderedBy
	&OrderedDsc = &GridState.OrderedDsc

	For &GridStateFilterValue in &GridState.FilterValues
		Do Case
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"FILTERFULLTEXT"
				&FilterFullText.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUEFECHA"
				&TFEmbarqueFecha.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
				&TFEmbarqueFecha_To.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.ValueTo)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECODIGO"
				&TFEmbarqueCodigo.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECODIGO_SEL"
				&TFEmbarqueCodigo_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECLIENTE"
				&TFEmbarqueCliente.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECLIENTE_SEL"
				&TFEmbarqueCliente_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECLIENTEGRUPO"
				&TFEmbarqueClienteGrupo.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECLIENTEGRUPO_SEL"
				&TFEmbarqueClienteGrupo_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECLIENTEENVIA"
				&TFEmbarqueClienteEnvia.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECLIENTEENVIA_SEL"
				&TFEmbarqueClienteEnvia_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUEDETALLEPRODUCTO"
				&TFEmbarqueDetalleProducto.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUEDETALLEPRODUCTO_SEL"
				&TFEmbarqueDetalleProducto_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUEPALLETNOPALLET"
				&TFEmbarquePalletNoPallet.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUEPALLETNOPALLET_SEL"
				&TFEmbarquePalletNoPallet_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
		EndCase
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

=== Procedure: Reportes.PalletEnEmbarqueExportReport ===

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

// Exports the contents of a grid (using the selected order and filters) to a PDF file.
&IsAuthorized = SecGAMIsAuthByFunctionalityKey.Udp(!'palletenembarque_Execute') 
If &IsAuthorized

	LoadWWPContext.Call(&WWPContext)

Do 'LoadGridState'

&Title = "Embarque Pallet List"

//Report Template Source
	Header
		&AppName = "DVelop Software Solutions"
		&Phone = !"+1 550 8923"
		&Mail = !"info@mail.com"
		&Website = !"http://www.web.com"
		&AddressLine1 = !"French Boulevard 2859"
		&AddressLine2 = !"Downtown"
		&AddressLine3 = !"Paris, France"
		Print printTitle
	End 
	Footer
		&PageInfo = "Page: " + &Page.ToString().Trim()
		&DateInfo = "Date: " + &Today.ToFormattedString()
		Print printFooter
	End

	Do 'PrintFilters'

	Do 'PrintColumnTitles'

	Do 'PrintData'

	Do 'PrintFooter'

EndIf

Sub 'PrintFilters'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	If not &FilterFullText.IsEmpty()
		print printBlockFilterFilterFullText
	EndIf
	If not (&TFEmbarqueFecha.IsEmpty() AND &TFEmbarqueFecha_To.IsEmpty())
		print printBlockTFEmbarqueFecha
		&TFEmbarqueFecha_To_Description = format('%1 (%2)', "Fecha", "WWP_TSTo")
		print printBlockTFEmbarqueFecha_To
	EndIf
	If not &TFEmbarqueCodigo_Sel.IsEmpty()
		print printBlockTFEmbarqueCodigo_Sel
	Else
		If not &TFEmbarqueCodigo.IsEmpty()
			print printBlockTFEmbarqueCodigo
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If not &TFEmbarqueCliente_Sel.IsEmpty()
		print printBlockTFEmbarqueCliente_Sel
	Else
		If not &TFEmbarqueCliente.IsEmpty()
			print printBlockTFEmbarqueCliente
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If not &TFEmbarqueClienteGrupo_Sel.IsEmpty()
		print printBlockTFEmbarqueClienteGrupo_Sel
	Else
		If not &TFEmbarqueClienteGrupo.IsEmpty()
			print printBlockTFEmbarqueClienteGrupo
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If not &TFEmbarqueClienteEnvia_Sel.IsEmpty()
		print printBlockTFEmbarqueClienteEnvia_Sel
	Else
		If not &TFEmbarqueClienteEnvia.IsEmpty()
			print printBlockTFEmbarqueClienteEnvia
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If not &TFEmbarqueDetalleProducto_Sel.IsEmpty()
		print printBlockTFEmbarqueDetalleProducto_Sel
	Else
		If not &TFEmbarqueDetalleProducto.IsEmpty()
			print printBlockTFEmbarqueDetalleProducto
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If not &TFEmbarquePalletNoPallet_Sel.IsEmpty()
		print printBlockTFEmbarquePalletNoPallet_Sel
	Else
		If not &TFEmbarquePalletNoPallet.IsEmpty()
			print printBlockTFEmbarquePalletNoPallet
		EndIf
	EndIf

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'PrintData'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	For each DB.EmbarquePallet
		order EmbarquePalletValido  when &OrderedBy = 1
		order EmbarqueFecha  when &OrderedBy = 2 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (EmbarqueFecha)  when &OrderedBy = 2 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order EmbarqueCliente  when &OrderedBy = 3 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (EmbarqueCliente)  when &OrderedBy = 3 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order EmbarqueClienteGrupo  when &OrderedBy = 4 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (EmbarqueClienteGrupo)  when &OrderedBy = 4 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order EmbarqueClienteEnvia  when &OrderedBy = 5 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (EmbarqueClienteEnvia)  when &OrderedBy = 5 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order EmbarqueDetalleProducto  when &OrderedBy = 6 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (EmbarqueDetalleProducto)  when &OrderedBy = 6 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order EmbarquePalletNoPallet  when &OrderedBy = 7 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (EmbarquePalletNoPallet)  when &OrderedBy = 7 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		
		using PalletEnEmbarqueDS(&FilterFullText, &TFEmbarqueFecha, &TFEmbarqueFecha_To, &TFEmbarqueCodigo, &TFEmbarqueCodigo_Sel, &TFEmbarqueCliente
					, &TFEmbarqueCliente_Sel, &TFEmbarqueClienteGrupo, &TFEmbarqueClienteGrupo_Sel, &TFEmbarqueClienteEnvia, &TFEmbarqueClienteEnvia_Sel, &TFEmbarqueDetalleProducto
					, &TFEmbarqueDetalleProducto_Sel, &TFEmbarquePalletNoPallet, &TFEmbarquePalletNoPallet_Sel)
		Where EmbarqueEstatus in (EstatusEmbarque.Finalizado)

		Do 'BeforePrintLine'
		print printBlockLines_data
		Do 'AfterPrintLine'
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'LoadGridState'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	// Load grid state from session.

		&GridState.FromXml(WWPBaseObjects.LoadGridState.Udp(!"Reportes.PalletEnEmbarqueGridState"))
	Else
		&GridState.FromXml(&Session.Get(!"Reportes.PalletEnEmbarqueGridState"))

				&TFEmbarqueFecha_To.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.ValueTo)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECODIGO"
				&TFEmbarqueCodigo.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECODIGO_SEL"
				&TFEmbarqueCodigo_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECLIENTE"
				&TFEmbarqueCliente.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECLIENTE_SEL"
				&TFEmbarqueCliente_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECLIENTEGRUPO"
				&TFEmbarqueClienteGrupo.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECLIENTEGRUPO_SEL"
				&TFEmbarqueClienteGrupo_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECLIENTEENVIA"
				&TFEmbarqueClienteEnvia.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECLIENTEENVIA_SEL"
				&TFEmbarqueClienteEnvia_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUEDETALLEPRODUCTO"
				&TFEmbarqueDetalleProducto.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUEDETALLEPRODUCTO_SEL"
				&TFEmbarqueDetalleProducto_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUEPALLETNOPALLET"
				&TFEmbarquePalletNoPallet.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUEPALLETNOPALLET_SEL"
				&TFEmbarquePalletNoPallet_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
		EndCase
	EndFor

=== Procedure: Reportes.PalletEnEmbarqueGetFilterData ===

--- Source (Source) ---

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

&Options = new()
&OptionsDesc = new()
&OptionIndexes = new()
LoadWWPContext.Call(&WWPContext)

Do 'LoadGridState'
Do Case
	Case &DDOName.ToUpper() = !'DDO_EMBARQUECODIGO'
		Do 'LoadEmbarqueCodigoOptions'
	Case &DDOName.ToUpper() = !'DDO_EMBARQUECLIENTE'
		Do 'LoadEmbarqueClienteOptions'
	Case &DDOName.ToUpper() = !'DDO_EMBARQUECLIENTEGRUPO'
		Do 'LoadEmbarqueClienteGrupoOptions'
	Case &DDOName.ToUpper() = !'DDO_EMBARQUECLIENTEENVIA'
		Do 'LoadEmbarqueClienteEnviaOptions'
	Case &DDOName.ToUpper() = !'DDO_EMBARQUEDETALLEPRODUCTO'
		Do 'LoadEmbarqueDetalleProductoOptions'
	Case &DDOName.ToUpper() = !'DDO_EMBARQUEPALLETNOPALLET'
		Do 'LoadEmbarquePalletNoPalletOptions'
EndCase

&OptionsJson = &Options.ToJson()
&OptionsDescJson = &OptionsDesc.ToJson()
&OptionIndexesJson = &OptionIndexes.ToJson()

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */


Sub 'LoadGridState'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	// Load grid state from session.
	If &Session.Get(!"Reportes.PalletEnEmbarqueGridState") = !""
		&GridState.FromXml(LoadGridState.Udp(!"Reportes.PalletEnEmbarqueGridState"))
	Else
		&GridState.FromXml(&Session.Get(!"Reportes.PalletEnEmbarqueGridState"))
	Endif	

	For &GridStateFilterValue in &GridState.FilterValues
		Do Case
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"FILTERFULLTEXT"
				&FilterFullText.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUEFECHA"
				&TFEmbarqueFecha.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
				&TFEmbarqueFecha_To.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.ValueTo)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECODIGO"
				&TFEmbarqueCodigo.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECODIGO_SEL"
				&TFEmbarqueCodigo_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECLIENTE"
				&TFEmbarqueCliente.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECLIENTE_SEL"
				&TFEmbarqueCliente_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECLIENTEGRUPO"
				&TFEmbarqueClienteGrupo.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECLIENTEGRUPO_SEL"
				&TFEmbarqueClienteGrupo_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECLIENTEENVIA"
				&TFEmbarqueClienteEnvia.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUECLIENTEENVIA_SEL"
				&TFEmbarqueClienteEnvia_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUEDETALLEPRODUCTO"
				&TFEmbarqueDetalleProducto.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUEDETALLEPRODUCTO_SEL"
				&TFEmbarqueDetalleProducto_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUEPALLETNOPALLET"
				&TFEmbarquePalletNoPallet.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFEMBARQUEPALLETNOPALLET_SEL"
				&TFEmbarquePalletNoPallet_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
		EndCase
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'LoadEmbarqueCodigoOptions'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&TFEmbarqueCodigo = &SearchTxt
	&TFEmbarqueCodigo_Sel.SetEmpty()
	For Each DB.EmbarquePallet
		using PalletEnEmbarqueDS(&FilterFullText, &TFEmbarqueFecha, &TFEmbarqueFecha_To, &TFEmbarqueCodigo, &TFEmbarqueCodigo_Sel, &TFEmbarqueCliente
					, &TFEmbarqueCliente_Sel, &TFEmbarqueClienteGrupo, &TFEmbarqueClienteGrupo_Sel, &TFEmbarqueClienteEnvia, &TFEmbarqueClienteEnvia_Sel, &TFEmbarqueDetalleProducto
					, &TFEmbarqueDetalleProducto_Sel, &TFEmbarquePalletNoPallet, &TFEmbarquePalletNoPallet_Sel)
		Where EmbarqueEstatus in (EstatusEmbarque.Finalizado)

		If not EmbarqueCodigo.IsEmpty()
			&Option = EmbarqueCodigo
			
			&InsertIndex = 1
			Do while &InsertIndex <= &Options.Count AND &Options.Item(&InsertIndex) < &Option
				&InsertIndex = &InsertIndex + 1
			EndDo
			If &InsertIndex <= &Options.Count AND &Options.Item(&InsertIndex) = &Option
				&count.FromString(&OptionIndexes.Item(&InsertIndex))
				&count += 1
				&OptionIndexes.Remove(&InsertIndex)
				&OptionIndexes.Add(trim(&count.ToFormattedString()), &InsertIndex)
			Else
				&Options.Add(&Option, &InsertIndex)
				&OptionIndexes.Add(!'1', &InsertIndex)
			EndIf
		EndIf
		If &Options.Count = 50
			Exit
		EndIf
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'LoadEmbarqueClienteOptions'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&TFEmbarqueCliente = &SearchTxt
	&TFEmbarqueCliente_Sel.SetEmpty()
	For Each DB.EmbarquePallet
		Order EmbarqueCliente
		using PalletEnEmbarqueDS(&FilterFullText, &TFEmbarqueFecha, &TFEmbarqueFecha_To, &TFEmbarqueCodigo, &TFEmbarqueCodigo_Sel, &TFEmbarqueCliente
					, &TFEmbarqueCliente_Sel, &TFEmbarqueClienteGrupo, &TFEmbarqueClienteGrupo_Sel, &TFEmbarqueClienteEnvia, &TFEmbarqueClienteEnvia_Sel, &TFEmbarqueDetalleProducto
					, &TFEmbarqueDetalleProducto_Sel, &TFEmbarquePalletNoPallet, &TFEmbarquePalletNoPallet_Sel)
		Where EmbarqueEstatus in (EstatusEmbarque.Finalizado)

		&count = 0
		For Each DB.EmbarquePallet
			Order EmbarqueCliente
			&count += 1
		EndFor
		If not EmbarqueCliente.IsEmpty()
			&Option = EmbarqueCliente
			
			&Options.Add(&Option)
			&OptionIndexes.Add(trim(&count.ToFormattedString()))
		EndIf
		If &Options.Count = 50
			Exit
		EndIf
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

					, &TFEmbarqueCliente_Sel, &TFEmbarqueClienteGrupo, &TFEmbarqueClienteGrupo_Sel, &TFEmbarqueClienteEnvia, &TFEmbarqueClienteEnvia_Sel, &TFEmbarqueDetalleProducto

		Where EmbarqueEstatus in (EstatusEmbarque.Finalizado)

		&count = 0

=== Transaction: Reportes.PrensaObservacion ===

--- Start (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	LoadWWPContext.Call(&WWPContext)
	&TrnContext.FromXml(&WebSession.Get(!"TrnContext"))
	If (&TrnContext.TransactionName = &Pgmname and &Mode = TrnMode.Insert)
		For &TrnContextAtt in &TrnContext.Attributes
			Do Case
				// When inserting with instantiated TurnoId
				Case &TrnContextAtt.AttributeName = !"TurnoId"
					&Insert_TurnoId.FromString(&TrnContextAtt.AttributeValue)
				// When inserting with instantiated PrensaId
				Case &TrnContextAtt.AttributeName = !"PrensaId"
					&Insert_PrensaId.FromString(&TrnContextAtt.AttributeValue)
			Endcase
		Endfor
	Endif
	
    [web]
    {
	PrensaObservacionId.Visible = False
	}
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- After Trn (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	AuditTransaction.Call(&AuditingObject,&pgmname)	
   [web]
   {
	
	If (&Mode = TrnMode.Delete and not &TrnContext.CallerOnDelete)
		PrensaObservacionWW.Call()
	Endif
	Return
   }
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- Rules (Rules) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
[web]
{
parm(in:&Mode, in:&PrensaObservacionId);

PrensaObservacionId = &PrensaObservacionId if not &PrensaObservacionId.IsEmpty();
noaccept(PrensaObservacionId);
noprompt(PrensaObservacionId);

TurnoId = &Insert_TurnoId if &Mode = TrnMode.Insert and not &Insert_TurnoId.IsEmpty();
noaccept(TurnoId) if &Mode = TrnMode.Insert and not &Insert_TurnoId.IsEmpty();
PrensaId = &Insert_PrensaId if &Mode = TrnMode.Insert and not &Insert_PrensaId.IsEmpty();
noaccept(PrensaId) if &Mode = TrnMode.Insert and not &Insert_PrensaId.IsEmpty();
}

LoadAuditPrensaObservacion.Call(!'Y', &AuditingObject, PrensaObservacionId, &Mode) on beforeUpdate;
LoadAuditPrensaObservacion.Call(!'Y', &AuditingObject, PrensaObservacionId, &Mode) on beforeDelete;
LoadAuditPrensaObservacion.Call(!'N', &AuditingObject, PrensaObservacionId, &Mode) If Insert on BeforeComplete;
LoadAuditPrensaObservacion.Call(!'N', &AuditingObject, PrensaObservacionId, &Mode) If Update on BeforeComplete;
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */


Default(PrensaObservacionFecha, Today());


=== WebPanel: Reportes.PrensaObservacionView ===

--- Start (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	LoadWWPContext.Call(&WWPContext)
	WorkWithLink.Link = PrensaObservacionWW.Link()
	For each PrensaObservacion
		where PrensaObservacionId = &PrensaObservacionId
		Form.Caption = PrensaObservacionFecha.ToString()
		&Exists = True
	When none
		Form.Caption = "WWP_RecordNotFound"
		&Exists = False
	Endfor
	If (&Exists)
		&SelectedTabCode = &TabCode
		Tabs.ActivePageControlName = &SelectedTabCode
		Do 'LoadFixedTabs'
		Do 'LoadTabs'
	Endif
	&RecordDescription = Form.Caption
	If &Session.Get(!'DiscussionThreadIdToOpen') <> ''
		ddc_Discussions.Open()
	EndIf
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

--- Refresh (Event) ---
/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */
	If Not WWP_HasDiscussionMessages(!'PrensaObservacion', PrensaObservacionId.ToString().Trim())
		ddc_Discussions.Icon = "far fa-comment"
	EndIf
	If (WWP_HasSubscriptionsToDisplay(!'PrensaObservacion', WWPBaseObjects.Notifications.WWP_NotificationAppliesTo.SpecificRecord))
		ddc_Subscriptions.Visible = True
	Else
		ddc_Subscriptions.Visible = False
	Endif
	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

=== Procedure: Reportes.PrensaObservacionWWExport ===

--- Source (Source) ---

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

// Exports the contents of a grid (using the selected order and filters) to an Excel file.
LoadWWPContext.Call(&WWPContext)

Do 'OpenDocument'

&CellRow = 1
&FirstColumn = 1

Do 'LoadGridState'

Do 'WriteFilters'

Do 'WriteColumnTitles'

Do 'WriteData'

Do 'CloseDocument'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */


Sub 'OpenDocument'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&Random = Random() * 10000
	&Filename = !"PrensaObservacionWWExport-" + &Random.ToString().Trim() + !".xlsx"

	&ExcelDocument.Open(&Filename)
	Do 'CheckStatus'
	&ExcelDocument.Clear()

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'WriteFilters'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	If not (&FilterFullText.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "WWP_FullTextFilterDescription")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&FilterFullText)
	EndIf
	If not (&TFPrensaObservacionFecha.IsEmpty() AND &TFPrensaObservacionFecha_To.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Fecha")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Date = &TFPrensaObservacionFecha
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, false, &CellRow, &FirstColumn + 2, "WWP_TSTo")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 3).Date = &TFPrensaObservacionFecha_To
	EndIf
	If not (&TFPrensaNombre_Sel.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Prensa")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFPrensaNombre_Sel)
	Else
		If not (&TFPrensaNombre.IsEmpty())
			WWPBaseObjects.WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Prensa")
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFPrensaNombre)
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If not (&TFTurnoNombre_Sel.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Turno")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFTurnoNombre_Sel)
	Else
		If not (&TFTurnoNombre.IsEmpty())
			WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Turno")
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFTurnoNombre)
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If not (&TFPrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion.IsEmpty() AND &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Interrupción (Hr)")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Number = &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, false, &CellRow, &FirstColumn + 2, "WWP_TSTo")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 3).Number = &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To
	EndIf
	If not (&TFPrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor.IsEmpty() AND &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To.IsEmpty())
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Tiempo (Hr)")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Number = &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor
		WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, false, &CellRow, &FirstColumn + 2, "WWP_TSTo")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 3).Number = &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To
	EndIf
	If not (&TFPrensaObservacionDescripcion_Sel.IsEmpty())
		WWPBaseObjects.WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Descripción")
		&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFPrensaObservacionDescripcion_Sel)
	Else
		If not (&TFPrensaObservacionDescripcion.IsEmpty())
			WWPBaseObjects.WWP_ExportWriteFilter(&ExcelDocument, true, &CellRow, &FirstColumn, "Descripción")
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + 1).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(&TFPrensaObservacionDescripcion)
		EndIf
	EndIf
	&CellRow += 2

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'WriteColumnTitles'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&VisibleColumnCount = 0
	If &Session.Get(!'Reportes.PrensaObservacionWWColumnsSelector') <> ''
		&ColumnsSelectorXML = &Session.Get(!'Reportes.PrensaObservacionWWColumnsSelector')
		&ColumnsSelector.FromXml(&ColumnsSelectorXML)
	Else
		Do 'InitializeColumnsSelector'
	EndIf

	&ColumnsSelector.Columns.Sort(!'Order')
	For &ColumnsSelector_Column in &ColumnsSelector.Columns
		If &ColumnsSelector_Column.IsVisible = True
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = GetMessageText(iif(&ColumnsSelector_Column.DisplayName.IsEmpty(), &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName, &ColumnsSelector_Column.DisplayName))
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Bold = True
			&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Color = 11
			&VisibleColumnCount += 1
		EndIf
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'WriteData'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	For each PrensaObservacion
		order PrensaObservacionFecha  when &OrderedBy = 1 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (PrensaObservacionFecha)  when &OrderedBy = 1 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order PrensaNombre  when &OrderedBy = 2 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (PrensaNombre)  when &OrderedBy = 2 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order TurnoNombre  when &OrderedBy = 3 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (TurnoNombre)  when &OrderedBy = 3 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order PrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion  when &OrderedBy = 4 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (PrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion)  when &OrderedBy = 4 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order PrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor  when &OrderedBy = 5 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (PrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor)  when &OrderedBy = 5 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		order PrensaObservacionDescripcion  when &OrderedBy = 6 AND &OrderedDsc = False
		order (PrensaObservacionDescripcion)  when &OrderedBy = 6 AND &OrderedDsc = True
		
		using PrensaObservacionWWDS(&FilterFullText, &TFPrensaObservacionFecha, &TFPrensaObservacionFecha_To, &TFPrensaNombre, &TFPrensaNombre_Sel, &TFTurnoNombre
					, &TFTurnoNombre_Sel, &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion, &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To, &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor, &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To, &TFPrensaObservacionDescripcion
					, &TFPrensaObservacionDescripcion_Sel)

		// Write cell values
		&CellRow += 1
		
		Do 'BeforeWriteLine'
		&VisibleColumnCount = 0
		For &ColumnsSelector_Column in &ColumnsSelector.Columns
			If &ColumnsSelector_Column.IsVisible = True
				Do Case
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'PrensaObservacionFecha'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Date = PrensaObservacionFecha
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'PrensaNombre'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = WWPBaseObjects.WWP_Export_SecureText(PrensaNombre)
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'TurnoNombre'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(TurnoNombre)
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'PrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Number = PrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'PrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Number = PrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor
					Case &ColumnsSelector_Column.ColumnName = !'PrensaObservacionDescripcion'
						&ExcelDocument.Cells(&CellRow, &FirstColumn + &VisibleColumnCount).Text = WWP_Export_SecureText(PrensaObservacionDescripcion)
				EndCase
				&VisibleColumnCount += 1
			EndIf
		EndFor		
		
		Do 'AfterWriteLine'

	Endfor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'CloseDocument'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&ExcelDocument.Save()
	Do 'CheckStatus'
	&ExcelDocument.Close()

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'CheckStatus'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	If (&ExcelDocument.ErrCode <> 0)
		&Filename = ""
		&ErrorMessage = &ExcelDocument.ErrDescription
		&ExcelDocument.Close()
		Return
	EndIf

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'InitializeColumnsSelector'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&ColumnsSelector = new WWPColumnsSelector()
	WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"PrensaObservacionFecha", '', !'Fecha', True, '')
	WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"PrensaNombre", '', !'Prensa', True, '')
	WWPBaseObjects.WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"TurnoNombre", '', !'Turno', True, '')
	WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"PrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion", '', !'Interrupción (Hr)', True, '')
	WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"PrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor", '', !'Tiempo (Hr)', True, '')
	WWP_ColumnsSelector_Add(&ColumnsSelector, !"PrensaObservacionDescripcion", '', !'Descripción', True, '')
		
	&UserCustomValue = LoadColumnsSelectorState.Udp(!'Reportes.PrensaObservacionWWColumnsSelector')
	If not(&UserCustomValue.IsEmpty())
		&ColumnsSelectorAux.FromXml(&UserCustomValue)
		WWP_ColumnSelector_UpdateColumns(&ColumnsSelectorAux, &ColumnsSelector)
	EndIf

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'LoadGridState'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	// Load grid state from session.
	If &Session.Get(!"Reportes.PrensaObservacionWWGridState") = !""
		&GridState.FromXml(LoadGridState.Udp(!"Reportes.PrensaObservacionWWGridState"))
	Else
		&GridState.FromXml(&Session.Get(!"Reportes.PrensaObservacionWWGridState"))
	Endif	

	&OrderedBy = &GridState.OrderedBy
	&OrderedDsc = &GridState.OrderedDsc

	For &GridStateFilterValue in &GridState.FilterValues
		Do Case
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"FILTERFULLTEXT"
				&FilterFullText.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPRENSAOBSERVACIONFECHA"
				&TFPrensaObservacionFecha.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
				&TFPrensaObservacionFecha_To.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.ValueTo)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPRENSANOMBRE"
				&TFPrensaNombre.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPRENSANOMBRE_SEL"
				&TFPrensaNombre_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFTURNONOMBRE"
				&TFTurnoNombre.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFTURNONOMBRE_SEL"
				&TFTurnoNombre_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPRENSAOBSERVACIONTIEMPOINTERRUPCION"
				&TFPrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
				&TFPrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.ValueTo)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPRENSAOBSERVACIONTIEMPOSUPERVISOR"
				&TFPrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
				&TFPrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.ValueTo)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPRENSAOBSERVACIONDESCRIPCION"
				&TFPrensaObservacionDescripcion.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPRENSAOBSERVACIONDESCRIPCION_SEL"
				&TFPrensaObservacionDescripcion_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
		EndCase
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */



	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

=== Procedure: Reportes.PrensaObservacionWWExportReport ===

--- Source (Source) ---

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

// Exports the contents of a grid (using the selected order and filters) to a PDF file.
&IsAuthorized = SecGAMIsAuthByFunctionalityKey.Udp(!'prensaobservacionww_Execute') 
If &IsAuthorized

	WWPBaseObjects.LoadWWPContext.Call(&WWPContext)

Do 'LoadGridState'

&Title = "Prensa Observacion List"

//Report Template Source
	Header
		&AppName = "DVelop Software Solutions"
		&Phone = !"+1 550 8923"
		&Mail = !"info@mail.com"
		&Website = !"http://www.web.com"
		&AddressLine1 = !"French Boulevard 2859"
		&AddressLine2 = !"Downtown"
		&AddressLine3 = !"Paris, France"
		Print printTitle
	End 
	Footer
		&PageInfo = "Page: " + &Page.ToString().Trim()
		&DateInfo = "Date: " + &Today.ToFormattedString()
		Print printFooter
	End

	Do 'PrintFilters'

	Do 'PrintColumnTitles'

	Do 'PrintData'

	Do 'PrintFooter'

EndIf

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */


Sub 'PrintFilters'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	If not &FilterFullText.IsEmpty()
		print printBlockFilterFilterFullText
	EndIf
	If not (&TFPrensaObservacionFecha.IsEmpty() AND &TFPrensaObservacionFecha_To.IsEmpty())
		print printBlockTFPrensaObservacionFecha
		&TFPrensaObservacionFecha_To_Description = format('%1 (%2)', "Fecha", "WWP_TSTo")
		print printBlockTFPrensaObservacionFecha_To
	EndIf
	If not &TFPrensaNombre_Sel.IsEmpty()
		print printBlockTFPrensaNombre_Sel
	Else
		If not &TFPrensaNombre.IsEmpty()
			print printBlockTFPrensaNombre
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If not &TFTurnoNombre_Sel.IsEmpty()
		print printBlockTFTurnoNombre_Sel
	Else
		If not &TFTurnoNombre.IsEmpty()
			print printBlockTFTurnoNombre
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If not (&TFPrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion.IsEmpty() AND &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To.IsEmpty())
		print printBlockTFPrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion
		&TFPrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To_Description = format('%1 (%2)', "Interrupción (Hr)", "WWP_TSTo")
		print printBlockTFPrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To
	EndIf
	If not (&TFPrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor.IsEmpty() AND &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To.IsEmpty())
		print printBlockTFPrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor
		&TFPrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To_Description = format('%1 (%2)', "Tiempo (Hr)", "WWP_TSTo")
		print printBlockTFPrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To
	EndIf
	If not &TFPrensaObservacionDescripcion_Sel.IsEmpty()
		print printBlockTFPrensaObservacionDescripcion_Sel
	Else
		If not &TFPrensaObservacionDescripcion.IsEmpty()
			print printBlockTFPrensaObservacionDescripcion
		EndIf
	EndIf

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

					, &TFPrensaObservacionDescripcion_Sel)

=== Procedure: Reportes.PrensaObservacionWWGetFilterData ===

--- Source (Source) ---

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

&Options = new()
&OptionsDesc = new()
&OptionIndexes = new()
LoadWWPContext.Call(&WWPContext)

Do 'LoadGridState'
Do Case
	Case &DDOName.ToUpper() = !'DDO_PRENSANOMBRE'
		Do 'LoadPrensaNombreOptions'
	Case &DDOName.ToUpper() = !'DDO_TURNONOMBRE'
		Do 'LoadTurnoNombreOptions'
	Case &DDOName.ToUpper() = !'DDO_PRENSAOBSERVACIONDESCRIPCION'
		Do 'LoadPrensaObservacionDescripcionOptions'
EndCase

&OptionsJson = &Options.ToJson()
&OptionsDescJson = &OptionsDesc.ToJson()
&OptionIndexesJson = &OptionIndexes.ToJson()

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */


Sub 'LoadGridState'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	// Load grid state from session.
	If &Session.Get(!"Reportes.PrensaObservacionWWGridState") = !""
		&GridState.FromXml(LoadGridState.Udp(!"Reportes.PrensaObservacionWWGridState"))
	Else
		&GridState.FromXml(&Session.Get(!"Reportes.PrensaObservacionWWGridState"))
	Endif	

	For &GridStateFilterValue in &GridState.FilterValues
		Do Case
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"FILTERFULLTEXT"
				&FilterFullText.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPRENSAOBSERVACIONFECHA"
				&TFPrensaObservacionFecha.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
				&TFPrensaObservacionFecha_To.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.ValueTo)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPRENSANOMBRE"
				&TFPrensaNombre.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPRENSANOMBRE_SEL"
				&TFPrensaNombre_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFTURNONOMBRE"
				&TFTurnoNombre.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFTURNONOMBRE_SEL"
				&TFTurnoNombre_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPRENSAOBSERVACIONTIEMPOINTERRUPCION"
				&TFPrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
				&TFPrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.ValueTo)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPRENSAOBSERVACIONTIEMPOSUPERVISOR"
				&TFPrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
				&TFPrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.ValueTo)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPRENSAOBSERVACIONDESCRIPCION"
				&TFPrensaObservacionDescripcion.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
			Case &GridStateFilterValue.Name = !"TFPRENSAOBSERVACIONDESCRIPCION_SEL"
				&TFPrensaObservacionDescripcion_Sel.FromString(&GridStateFilterValue.Value)
		EndCase
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'LoadPrensaNombreOptions'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&TFPrensaNombre = &SearchTxt
	&TFPrensaNombre_Sel.SetEmpty()
	For Each PrensaObservacion
		Order PrensaId
		using PrensaObservacionWWDS(&FilterFullText, &TFPrensaObservacionFecha, &TFPrensaObservacionFecha_To, &TFPrensaNombre, &TFPrensaNombre_Sel, &TFTurnoNombre
					, &TFTurnoNombre_Sel, &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion, &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To, &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor, &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To, &TFPrensaObservacionDescripcion
					, &TFPrensaObservacionDescripcion_Sel)

		&count = 0
		For Each PrensaObservacion
			Order PrensaId
			&count += 1
		EndFor
		If not PrensaNombre.IsEmpty()
			&Option = PrensaNombre
			
			&InsertIndex = 1
			Do while &InsertIndex <= &Options.Count AND &Options.Item(&InsertIndex) < &Option
				&InsertIndex = &InsertIndex + 1
			EndDo
			&Options.Add(&Option, &InsertIndex)
			&OptionIndexes.Add(trim(&count.ToFormattedString()), &InsertIndex)
		EndIf
		If &Options.Count = 50
			Exit
		EndIf
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'LoadTurnoNombreOptions'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&TFTurnoNombre = &SearchTxt
	&TFTurnoNombre_Sel.SetEmpty()
	For Each PrensaObservacion
		Order TurnoId
		using PrensaObservacionWWDS(&FilterFullText, &TFPrensaObservacionFecha, &TFPrensaObservacionFecha_To, &TFPrensaNombre, &TFPrensaNombre_Sel, &TFTurnoNombre
					, &TFTurnoNombre_Sel, &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion, &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To, &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor, &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To, &TFPrensaObservacionDescripcion
					, &TFPrensaObservacionDescripcion_Sel)

		&count = 0
		For Each PrensaObservacion
			Order TurnoId
			&count += 1
		EndFor
		If not TurnoNombre.IsEmpty()
			&Option = TurnoNombre
			
			&InsertIndex = 1
			Do while &InsertIndex <= &Options.Count AND &Options.Item(&InsertIndex) < &Option
				&InsertIndex = &InsertIndex + 1
			EndDo
			&Options.Add(&Option, &InsertIndex)
			&OptionIndexes.Add(trim(&count.ToFormattedString()), &InsertIndex)
		EndIf
		If &Options.Count = 50
			Exit
		EndIf
	EndFor

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [End] - Do not change */

EndSub

Sub 'LoadPrensaObservacionDescripcionOptions'

	/* Generated by DVelop Work With Plus Pattern [Start] - Do not change */

	&TFPrensaObservacionDescripcion = &SearchTxt
	&TFPrensaObservacionDescripcion_Sel.SetEmpty()
	For Each Reportes.PrensaObservacion
		Order PrensaObservacionDescripcion
		using PrensaObservacionWWDS(&FilterFullText, &TFPrensaObservacionFecha, &TFPrensaObservacionFecha_To, &TFPrensaNombre, &TFPrensaNombre_Sel, &TFTurnoNombre
					, &TFTurnoNombre_Sel, &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion, &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoInterrupcion_To, &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor, &TFPrensaObservacionTiempoSupervisor_To, &TFPrensaObservacionDescripcion
					, &TFPrensaObservacionDescripcion_Sel)

		&count = 0
		For Each PrensaObservacion
			Order PrensaObservacionDescripcion
			&count += 1
		EndFor
		If not PrensaObservacionDescripcion.IsEmpty()
			&Option = PrensaObservacionDescripcion
			
			&Options.Add(&Option)
			&OptionIndexes.Add(trim(&count.ToFormattedString()))
		EndIf
		If &Options.Count = 50
			Exit
		EndIf
	EndFor

EndSub
